            local_a_i = line_arc_intersection2(q, local_p_i, 0.5, [q, b2[2]], True, False, True)
            if local_a_i == 0:
                if printInfo:
                    print(f"local_a_i: {local_a_i}")
                    print(
                        "Something weird with lune prune for a_i, there should be a singular intersection between an edge and the arc!")
                    print(b1)
                    print(b2)
                    print("Have an intersection for line")
                    print(q)
                    print(local_p_i)
                    print("With arc")
                    print([q, b2[2]])
                    print("and intersection")
                    print(local_a_i)
                    sys.exit()

                error = -1
                return error

            # if there are two intersection points, output the one that's further from the pseudoterminal
            if len(local_a_i) > 1:

                first_intersect = local_a_i[0]
                second_intersect = local_a_i[1]

                first_dist = dist(first_intersect, q)
                second_dist = dist(second_intersect, q)

                if second_dist > first_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_a_i = [local_a_i[1]]
                elif first_dist > second_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_a_i = [local_a_i[0]]
                else:
                    print(f"the two intersections are too close")
                    sys.exit()

            # Extract the single point
            local_a_i = local_a_i[0]
            local_alpha_a_i = angle(local_a_i, o_ac_q) - angle(a, o_ac_q)
            if local_alpha_a_i < -EPS2:
                local_alpha_a_i = local_alpha_a_i + 2 * np.pi

        # Now need to find a terminal which violates the lune property
        z = None

        for terminal in terminals:
            localDistance1 = dist(terminal, local_a_i)
            localDistance2 = dist(terminal, local_p_i)
            edgeDistance = dist(local_a_i, local_p_i)
            if (abs(localDistance1 - edgeDistance) > 0.001 and
                    abs(localDistance2 - edgeDistance) > 0.001 and
                    localDistance1 < edgeDistance and
                    localDistance2 < edgeDistance):
                z = terminal
                break
        # Have no terminals which violate the lune property, get out of the loop
        if z == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No further lune violations, stopping!")
            break
        # If here, have a terminal z which violates the lune property. Get to work!
        bigA = np.sqrt(3) * r_pq * math.cos(local_alpha_p_i / 2) - r_pq * math.sin(local_alpha_p_i / 2) - np.sqrt(
            3) * r_ac_q * math.cos(local_alpha_a_i / 2) - r_ac_q * math.sin(local_alpha_a_i / 2)
        bigB = np.sqrt(3) * r_pq * math.sin(local_alpha_p_i / 2) + r_pq * math.cos(local_alpha_p_i / 2) - np.sqrt(
            3) * r_ac_q * math.sin(local_alpha_a_i / 2) + r_ac_q * math.cos(local_alpha_a_i / 2)
        bigC = dist(z, o_ac_q) * dist(z, o_ac_q) + r_ac_q * r_ac_q
        bigD = -2 * ((z[0] - o_ac_q[0]) * (local_a_i[0] - o_ac_q[0]) + (z[1] - o_ac_q[1]) * (local_a_i[1] - o_ac_q[1]))
        bigE = -2 * ((z[0] - o_ac_q[0]) * (local_a_i[1] - o_ac_q[1]) - (z[1] - o_ac_q[1]) * (local_a_i[0] - o_ac_q[0]))
        bigF = bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2 - bigC
        bigG = bigE - bigA * bigB
        bigH = bigD - bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2
        # Get the beta solutions
        # Have the beta solution, store as the local beta for the |z a'_i| test
        solvedAngles = solve_angle(bigF, bigG, bigH)
        if solvedAngles == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No solutions, returning 0!")
            return 0

        local_beta_a_dash_i = min(solvedAngles)
        # Repeat with p'_i
        bigC = dist(z, o_pq) * dist(z, o_pq) + r_pq * r_pq
        bigD = -2 * ((z[0] - o_pq[0]) * (local_p_i[0] - o_pq[0]) + (z[1] - o_pq[1]) * (local_p_i[1] - o_pq[1]))
        bigE = -2 * ((z[0] - o_pq[0]) * (local_p_i[1] - o_pq[1]) - (z[1] - o_pq[1]) * (local_p_i[0] - o_pq[0]))
        bigF = bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2 - bigC
        bigG = bigE - bigA * bigB
        bigH = bigD - bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2
        # Get the beta solutions
        # Have the beta solution, store as the local beta for the |z p'_i| test
        solvedAngles = solve_angle(bigF, bigG, bigH)
        if solvedAngles == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No solutions, returning 0!")
            return 0
        local_beta_p_dash_i = min(solvedAngles)
        # Now have two betas, want the smaller of the two
        local_beta_p_i = local_beta_p_i + min(local_beta_p_dash_i, local_beta_a_dash_i)

        # Should also have a test here to make sure the maximum allowed beta is not reached
        if local_beta_p_i > max_opening:
            if printInfo:
                print("Need a beta of", local_beta_p_i, "which exceeds max opening")
            return 0

        # Sanity check: Based on this beta, should have a new p_i and a_i for which the lune test does
        # not fail, as z is on the boundary of the region.
        local_p_i = [arc_center[0] + arc_radius * math.cos(angles[2] + local_beta_p_i),
                     arc_center[1] + arc_radius * math.sin(angles[2] + local_beta_p_i)]
        local_a_i = q
        if r_ac_q != 0:

            # This should be an intersection of the line q p_i with the arc
            local_a_i = line_arc_intersection2(q, local_p_i, 0.5, [q, b2[2]], True, False, True)

            if local_a_i == 0:
                if printInfo:
                    print(
                        "Something weird with lune prune for a_i, there should be a singular intersection between an edge and the arc!")
                    print(b1)
                    print(b2)
                    print("Have an intersection for line")
                    print(q)
                    print(local_p_i)
                    print("With arc")
                    print([q, b2[2]])
                    print("and intersection")
                    print(local_a_i)
                    sys.exit()

                error = -1
                return error

            # if there are two intersection points, output the one that's further from the pseudoterminal
            if len(local_a_i) > 1:

                first_intersect = local_a_i[0]
                second_intersect = local_a_i[1]

                first_dist = dist(first_intersect, q)
                second_dist = dist(second_intersect, q)

                if second_dist > first_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_a_i = [local_a_i[1]]
                elif first_dist > second_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_a_i = [local_a_i[0]]
                else:
                    print(f"the two intersections are too close")
                    sys.exit()

            # Extract the single point
            local_a_i = local_a_i[0]
        # Should have been done so that z now lies in the border of the lune, i.e. its distance to one of the points is the same as the segment length
        if abs(dist(local_a_i, local_p_i) - dist(z, local_p_i)) > 0.001 and abs(
                dist(local_a_i, local_p_i) - dist(z, local_a_i)) > 0.001:
            if printInfo:
                print(
                    "Something weird with lune prune, new distances indicate z is not in the border of the updated lune!")
                print("Did lune test part 1, updated beta to", local_beta_p_i, "local distances are now: segment",
                      dist(local_a_i, local_p_i), ", p_i to z", dist(z, local_p_i), "and a_i to z", dist(local_a_i, z))
            error = -1
            return error

        if printInfo:
            print("Did lune test part 1, updated beta to", local_beta_p_i, "local distances are now: segment",
                  dist(local_a_i, local_p_i), ", p_i to z", dist(z, local_p_i), "and a_i to z", dist(local_a_i, z))
            # And then the loop goes again!

    # Continue with rotation on the p (by moving q_i)
    # Simply want to find a terminal which lies in the current lune. If no such terminal can be found, move on
    local_beta_q_i = 0
    while True:
        if printInfo:
            print("Look at moving q_i")
        # Figure out location of q_i and c_i given local beta

        local_q_i = [arc_center[0] + arc_radius * math.cos(angles[3] - local_beta_q_i),
                     arc_center[1] + arc_radius * math.sin(angles[3] - local_beta_q_i)]
        local_alpha_q_i = angle(q, o_pq) - angle(local_q_i, o_pq)
        if pruneUsingb1 == False:
            break
        if local_alpha_q_i < -EPS2:
            local_alpha_q_i = local_alpha_q_i + 2 * np.pi
        # Need to figure out c_i only if p is a pseudoterminal (and not an actual terminal)
        # If it is a terminal, set default values for angles and c's, as all will be removed with r_ac_p = 0.
        local_alpha_c_i = 0
        c = p
        local_c_i = p
        # If q is not an actual terminal, need to find the location of a and a_i
        if r_ac_p != 0:
            c = [
                (p[0] - o_ac_p[0]) * math.cos(4 * np.pi / 3) - (p[1] - o_ac_p[1]) * math.sin(4 * np.pi / 3) + o_ac_p[0],
                (p[0] - o_ac_p[0]) * math.sin(4 * np.pi / 3) + (p[1] - o_ac_p[1]) * math.cos(4 * np.pi / 3) + o_ac_p[1]]
            # This should be an intersection of the line q p_i with the arc
            local_c_i = line_arc_intersection2(p, local_q_i, 0.5, [p, b1[2]], True, False, True)
            if local_c_i == 0:
                if printInfo:
                    print(
                        "Something weird with lune prune for c_i, there should be a singular intersection between an edge and the arc!")
                    print(b1)
                    print(b2)
                    print("Have an intersection for line")
                    print(p)
                    print(local_q_i)
                    print("With arc")
                    print([p, b1[2]])
                    print("and intersection")
                    print(local_c_i)
                    sys.exit()

                error = -1
                return error

            # if there are two intersection points, output the one that's further from the pseudoterminal
            if len(local_c_i) > 1:

                first_intersect = local_c_i[0]
                second_intersect = local_c_i[1]

                first_dist = dist(first_intersect, p)
                second_dist = dist(second_intersect, p)

                if second_dist > first_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_c_i = [local_c_i[1]]
                elif first_dist > second_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_c_i = [local_c_i[0]]
                else:
                    print(f"the two intersections are too close")
                    sys.exit()

            # Extract the single point
            local_c_i = local_c_i[0]
            local_alpha_c_i = angle(c, o_ac_p) - angle(local_c_i, o_ac_p)

            if local_alpha_c_i < -EPS2:
                local_alpha_c_i = local_alpha_c_i + 2 * np.pi

        # Now need to find a terminal which violates the lune property
        z = None
        for terminal in terminals:
            localDistance1 = dist(terminal, local_c_i)
            localDistance2 = dist(terminal, local_q_i)
            edgeDistance = dist(local_c_i, local_q_i)
            if (abs(localDistance1 - edgeDistance) > 0.001 and
                    abs(localDistance2 - edgeDistance) > 0.001 and
                    localDistance1 < edgeDistance and
                    localDistance2 < edgeDistance):
                z = terminal
                break
        # Have no terminals which violate the lune property, get out of the loop
        if z == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No further lune violations, stopping!")
            break

        # If here, have a terminal z which violates the lune property. Get to work!
        bigA = np.sqrt(3) * r_pq * math.cos(local_alpha_q_i / 2) - r_pq * math.sin(local_alpha_q_i / 2) - np.sqrt(
            3) * r_ac_p * math.cos(local_alpha_c_i / 2) - r_ac_p * math.sin(local_alpha_c_i / 2)
        bigB = np.sqrt(3) * r_pq * math.sin(local_alpha_q_i / 2) + r_pq * math.cos(local_alpha_q_i / 2) - np.sqrt(
            3) * r_ac_p * math.sin(local_alpha_c_i / 2) + r_ac_p * math.cos(local_alpha_c_i / 2)
        bigC = dist(z, o_ac_p) * dist(z, o_ac_p) + r_ac_p * r_ac_p
        bigD = -2 * ((z[0] - o_ac_p[0]) * (local_c_i[0] - o_ac_p[0]) + (z[1] - o_ac_p[1]) * (local_c_i[1] - o_ac_p[1]))
        # Note the lack of a -1 at the front as the turn to c'_i is CLOCKWISE (so it is a - beta turn)
        bigE = 2 * ((z[0] - o_ac_p[0]) * (local_c_i[1] - o_ac_p[1]) - (z[1] - o_ac_p[1]) * (local_c_i[0] - o_ac_p[0]))
        bigF = bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2 - bigC
        bigG = bigE - bigA * bigB
        bigH = bigD - bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2
        # Get the beta solutions
        # Have the beta solution, store as the local beta for the |z c'_i| test
        solvedAngles = solve_angle(bigF, bigG, bigH)
        if solvedAngles == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No solutions, returning 0!")
            return 0
        local_beta_c_dash_i = min(solvedAngles)

        # Repeat with q'_i
        bigC = dist(z, o_pq) * dist(z, o_pq) + r_pq * r_pq
        bigD = -2 * ((z[0] - o_pq[0]) * (local_q_i[0] - o_pq[0]) + (z[1] - o_pq[1]) * (local_q_i[1] - o_pq[1]))
        bigE = 2 * ((z[0] - o_pq[0]) * (local_q_i[1] - o_pq[1]) - (z[1] - o_pq[1]) * (local_q_i[0] - o_pq[0]))
        bigF = bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2 - bigC
        bigG = bigE - bigA * bigB
        bigH = bigD - bigA * bigA / 2 + bigB * bigB / 2
        # Get the beta solutions
        # Have the beta solution, store as the local beta for the |z q'_i| test

        solvedAngles = solve_angle(bigF, bigG, bigH)
        if solvedAngles == None:
            if printInfo:
                print("No solutions, returning 0!")
            return 0
        local_beta_q_dash_i = min(solvedAngles)
        # Now have two betas, apparently want the smaller of the two
        local_beta_q_i = local_beta_q_i + min(local_beta_q_dash_i, local_beta_c_dash_i)

        # Should also have a test here to make sure the maximum allowed beta is not reached
        if local_beta_q_i + local_beta_p_i > max_opening:
            if printInfo:
                print("Need a beta of", local_beta_q_i, "plus found beta of", local_beta_p_i,
                      "which exceeds max opening")
            return 0
        # Sanity check: Based on this beta, should have a new q_i and c_i for which the lune test does
        # not fail, as z is on the boundary of the region.
        local_q_i = [arc_center[0] + arc_radius * math.cos(angles[3] - local_beta_q_i),
                     arc_center[1] + arc_radius * math.sin(angles[3] - local_beta_q_i)]
        local_c_i = p
        # If q is not an actual terminal, need to find the location of a and a_i
        if r_ac_p != 0:
            # This should be an intersection of the line q p_i with the arc
            local_c_i = line_arc_intersection2(p, local_q_i, 0.5, [p, b1[2]], True, False, True)
            if local_c_i == 0:
                if printInfo:
                    print(
                        "Something weird with lune prune for c_i, there should be a singular intersection between an edge and the arc!")
                    print(b1)
                    print(b2)
                    print("Have an intersection for line")
                    print(p)
                    print(local_q_i)
                    print("With arc")
                    print([p, b1[2]])
                    print("and intersection")
                    print(local_c_i)
                    sys.exit()

                error = -1
                return error

            # if there are two intersection points, output the one that's further from the pseudoterminal
            if len(local_c_i) > 1:

                first_intersect = local_c_i[0]
                second_intersect = local_c_i[1]

                first_dist = dist(first_intersect, p)
                second_dist = dist(second_intersect, p)

                if second_dist > first_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_c_i = [local_c_i[1]]
                elif first_dist > second_dist + 10 ** (-6):
                    local_c_i = [local_c_i[0]]
                else:
                    print(f"the two intersections are too close")
                    sys.exit()

            # Extract the single point
            local_c_i = local_c_i[0]

        # Should have been done so that z now lies in the border of the lune, i.e. its distance to one of the points is the same as the segment length
        if abs(dist(local_c_i, local_q_i) - dist(z, local_q_i)) > 0.001 and abs(
                dist(local_c_i, local_q_i) - dist(z, local_c_i)) > 0.001:
            if printInfo:
                print("Something weird with lune prune, new distances indicate z is not in the border of the updated lune!")
                print("Did lune test part 2, updated beta to", local_beta_q_i, "local distances are now: segment",
                      dist(local_c_i, local_q_i), ", q_i to z", dist(z, local_q_i), "and c_i to z", dist(local_c_i, z))
            error = -1
            return error

        if printInfo:
            print("Did lune test part 2, updated beta to", local_beta_q_i, "local distances are now: segment",
                  dist(local_c_i, local_q_i), ", q_i to z", dist(z, local_q_i), "and c_i to z", dist(local_c_i, z))

        # And then the loop goes again!

    # Now want the new set of angles based on these positions
    new_angles = [angle(local_p_i, pseudoterminal), angle(local_q_i, pseudoterminal), angle(local_p_i, o_pq),
                  angle(local_q_i, o_pq)]
    return new_angles


def double_bottleneck_test(b1, b2, full_new_arc):
    new_pseudo = full_new_arc[0]
    new_arc = full_new_arc[1]

    #  input is double_bottleneck_test(b1, b2, [new_pseudo, new_arc])

    # b1 pseudoterminal is a terminal
    if len(b1[2]) == 7 and b1[2][2] == 0:

        bottleneck_bound = bottleneck_between_sets(b1[3], b2[3])
        # bottleneck_bound = bottleneck_bound_array[b1[3][0]][b2[3][0]]

        result_a = double_bottleneck_zero_test(b1, b2, full_new_arc, bottleneck_bound, "a")

        if result_a == 0:
            return 0
        elif result_a != 1:
            new_arc[4] = result_a[0]
            new_arc[6] = result_a[1]

    # b2 pseudoterminal is a terminal
    if len(b2[2]) == 7 and b2[2][2] == 0:

        bottleneck_bound = bottleneck_between_sets(b2[3], b1[3])
        # bottleneck_bound = bottleneck_bound_array[b1[3][0]][b2[3][0]]

        result_b = double_bottleneck_zero_test(b1, b2, full_new_arc, bottleneck_bound, "b")

        if result_b == 0:
            return 0
        elif result_b != 1:
            new_arc[3] = result_b[0]
            new_arc[5] = result_b[1]

    return new_arc


def double_bottleneck_zero_test(b1, b2, full_new_arc, bottleneck_bound, a_or_b):
    new_pseudo = full_new_arc[0]
    new_arc = full_new_arc[1]
    centre = [new_arc[0], new_arc[1]]

    if a_or_b == "a":

        arc_r = [new_arc[0] + new_arc[2] * math.cos(new_arc[5]), new_arc[1] + new_arc[2] * math.sin(new_arc[5])]
        angle_r = angle_three(b1[1], centre, arc_r)

        r_chord = 2 * new_arc[2] * math.sin(angle_r / 2)

        if r_chord > bottleneck_bound:
            return 0

        arc_l = [new_arc[0] + new_arc[2] * math.cos(new_arc[6]), new_arc[1] + new_arc[2] * math.sin(new_arc[6])]
        angle_l = angle_three(b1[1], centre, arc_l)

        l_chord = 2 * new_arc[2] * math.sin(angle_l / 2)

        if l_chord > bottleneck_bound:
            new_l_angle = 2 * math.asin(bottleneck_bound / (2 * new_arc[2]))
            new_l = rotate(centre, b1[1], new_l_angle)

            new_l_pseudoangle = angle(new_l, new_pseudo)
            new_l_centreangle = angle(new_l, centre)

            return [new_l_pseudoangle, new_l_centreangle]

        return 1

    # a_or_b == "b"
    else:

        arc_l = [new_arc[0] + new_arc[2] * math.cos(new_arc[6]), new_arc[1] + new_arc[2] * math.sin(new_arc[6])]
        angle_l = angle_three(b2[1], centre, arc_l)

        l_chord = 2 * new_arc[2] * math.sin(angle_l / 2)

        if l_chord > bottleneck_bound:
            return 0

        arc_r = [new_arc[0] + new_arc[2] * math.cos(new_arc[5]), new_arc[1] + new_arc[2] * math.sin(new_arc[5])]
        angle_r = angle_three(b2[1], centre, arc_r)

        r_chord = 2 * new_arc[2] * math.sin(angle_r / 2)

        if r_chord > bottleneck_bound:
            new_r_angle = 2 * math.asin(bottleneck_bound / (2 * new_arc[2]))
            new_r = rotate(centre, b2[1], 2 * math.pi - new_r_angle)

            new_r_pseudoangle = angle(new_r, new_pseudo)
            new_r_centreangle = angle(new_r, centre)

            return [new_r_pseudoangle, new_r_centreangle]

        return 1


def triple_merge_checker(b1, b2, b3, number_of_steiner_pts):
    eps = 0.00001

    pseudo_1 = b1[1]
    pseudo_2 = b2[1]
    pseudo_3 = b3[1]

    vector_12 = [pseudo_2[x] - pseudo_1[x] for x in range(2)]
    vector_13 = [pseudo_3[x] - pseudo_1[x] for x in range(2)]

    sign = np.cross(vector_12, vector_13).tolist()

    # pseudoterminals 1, 2 and 3 are collinear and cannot be triple merged

    if abs(sign) < eps:
        return 0

    elif sign > 0:
        order = [[1, 0, 2], [2, 1, 0], [0, 2, 1]]

    else:
        order = [[2, 0, 1], [0, 1, 2], [1, 2, 0]]

    if rhombus_on == True and number_of_steiner_pts == 1:

        possible_orders = []
        dist12 = dist(pseudo_1, pseudo_2)
        dist13 = dist(pseudo_1, pseudo_3)
        dist23 = dist(pseudo_2, pseudo_3)
        distances = [dist23, dist13, dist12]

        max_dist = max(distances)

        for source_index, possible_order in enumerate(order):
            if distances[source_index] == max_dist:
                possible_orders.append(source_index)

        order = [order[x] for x in possible_orders]

    return order


def cone_intersection(cone1_angle1, cone1_angle2, cone2_angle1, cone2_angle2):
    if cone1_angle2 >= cone1_angle1 and cone2_angle2 >= cone2_angle1:
        lower = max(cone2_angle1, cone1_angle1)
        upper = min(cone2_angle2, cone1_angle2)
        if lower > upper:
            return 0
        else:
            final_1 = lower
            final_2 = upper

    elif cone1_angle2 >= cone1_angle1:
        lower_1 = max(cone1_angle1, cone2_angle1)
        upper_1 = min(cone1_angle2, cone2_angle2 + 2 * np.pi)
        if upper_1 >= lower_1:
            final_1 = lower_1
            final_2 = upper_1
        else:
            lower_2 = max(cone1_angle1, cone2_angle1 - 2 * np.pi)
            upper_2 = min(cone1_angle2, cone2_angle2)

            if lower_2 > upper_2:
                return 0
            else:
                final_1 = lower_2
                final_2 = upper_2

    elif cone2_angle2 >= cone2_angle1:
        lower_1 = max(cone2_angle1, cone1_angle1)
        upper_1 = min(cone2_angle2, cone1_angle2 + 2 * np.pi)
        if upper_1 >= lower_1:
            final_1 = lower_1
            final_2 = upper_1
        else:
            lower_2 = max(cone2_angle1, cone1_angle1 - 2 * np.pi)
            upper_2 = min(cone2_angle2, cone1_angle2)

            if lower_2 > upper_2:
                return 0
            else:
                final_1 = lower_2
                final_2 = upper_2

    else:
        final_1 = max(cone2_angle1, cone1_angle1)
        final_2 = min(cone2_angle2, cone1_angle2)

    return [final_1, final_2]


def triple_projection(b1, b2, b3):
    # checks whether two branches can be termination merged

    # interval a is an arc
    if len(b1[2]) == 7:
        # b1 is a terminal
        if b1[2][2] == 0:
            steiner_location_1 = b1[1]
        else:
            steiner_location_1 = line_arc_intersection(b1[1], b3[1], 0, [b1[1], b1[2]])
        if steiner_location_1 == 0:
            return 0

    # interval a is a segment
    else:
        steiner_location_1 = line_intersection([b1[1], b3[1]], [[b1[2][0], b1[2][1]], [b1[2][2], b1[2][3]]], 0, 0)
        if steiner_location_1 == 0:
            return 0

    # interval b is an arc
    if len(b3[2]) == 7:
        # b3 is a terminal
        if b3[2][2] == 0:
            steiner_location_2 = b3[1]
        else:
            steiner_location_2 = line_arc_intersection(b3[1], b1[1], 0, [b3[1], b3[2]])
        if steiner_location_2 == 0:
            return 0

    # interval b is a segment
    else:
        steiner_location_2 = line_intersection([b1[1], b3[1]], [[b3[2][0], b3[2][1]], [b3[2][2], b3[2][3]]], 0, 0)
        if steiner_location_2 == 0:
            return 0

    angle_1 = angle(steiner_location_1, b2[1])
    angle_2 = angle(steiner_location_2, b2[1])

    # steiner 1 should be closer to the b1 pseudoterminal than steiner 2
    if dist(steiner_location_1, b1[1]) > dist(steiner_location_2, b1[1]):
        return 0

    # steiner 2 should be closer to the b3 pseudoterminal than steiner 1
    if dist(steiner_location_2, b3[1]) > dist(steiner_location_1, b3[1]):
        return 0

    # if source interval is a segment
    if len(b2[2]) == 8:
        vector_seg = [steiner_location_2[0] - steiner_location_1[0], steiner_location_2[1] - steiner_location_1[1]]
        vector_pseudo = [b2[1][0] - steiner_location_1[0], b2[1][1] - steiner_location_1[1]]
        vector_end1 = [b2[2][0] - steiner_location_1[0], b2[2][1] - steiner_location_1[1]]
        vector_end2 = [b2[2][2] - steiner_location_1[0], b2[2][3] - steiner_location_1[1]]

        sign_pseudo = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_pseudo)
        sign_end1 = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_end1)
        sign_end2 = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_end2)

        # both endpoints of the source segment lie on the other side to the source pseudoterminal
        if sign_pseudo * sign_end1 <= 0 and sign_pseudo * sign_end2 <= 0:
            return 0

        # both endpoints of the source segment lie on the same side as the source pseudoterminal
        elif sign_pseudo * sign_end1 > 0 and sign_pseudo * sign_end2 > 0:
            source_angle_1 = angle([b2[2][0], b2[2][1]], b2[1])
            source_angle_2 = angle([b2[2][2], b2[2][3]], b2[1])

        # endpoint 1 on the correct side
        elif sign_pseudo * sign_end1 > 0:
            int_p = line_intersection([steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2],
                                      [[b2[2][0], b2[2][1]], [b2[2][2], b2[2][3]]], 2, 0)
            source_angle_1 = angle([b2[2][0], b2[2][1]], b2[1])
            source_angle_2 = angle(int_p, b2[1])

        # endpoint 2 on the correct side
        else:
            int_p = line_intersection([steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2],
                                      [[b2[2][0], b2[2][1]], [b2[2][2], b2[2][3]]], 2, 0)
            source_angle_1 = angle(int_p, b2[1])
            source_angle_2 = angle([b2[2][2], b2[2][3]], b2[1])

    # if source interval is an arc
    else:
        if b2[2][2] == 0:
            return [[steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2],
                    [steiner_location_1[0], steiner_location_1[1], steiner_location_2[0], steiner_location_2[1]]]

        vector_seg = [steiner_location_2[0] - steiner_location_1[0], steiner_location_2[1] - steiner_location_1[1]]
        vector_pseudo = [b2[1][0] - steiner_location_1[0], b2[1][1] - steiner_location_1[1]]

        location_end1 = [b2[2][0] + b2[2][2] * math.cos(b2[2][5]), b2[2][1] + b2[2][2] * math.sin(b2[2][5])]
        location_end2 = [b2[2][0] + b2[2][2] * math.cos(b2[2][6]), b2[2][1] + b2[2][2] * math.sin(b2[2][6])]

        vector_end1 = [location_end1[0] - steiner_location_1[0], location_end1[1] - steiner_location_1[1]]
        vector_end2 = [location_end2[0] - steiner_location_1[0], location_end2[1] - steiner_location_1[1]]

        sign_pseudo = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_pseudo)
        sign_end1 = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_end1)
        sign_end2 = np.cross(vector_seg, vector_end2)

        # both endpoints of the source arc lie on the other side to the source pseudoterminal
        if sign_pseudo * sign_end1 <= 0 and sign_pseudo * sign_end2 <= 0:
            return 0

        # endpoint 1 on the correct side
        elif sign_pseudo * sign_end1 > 0 and sign_pseudo * sign_end2 <= 0:

            # there should be one intersection point
            int_p = line_arc_intersection(steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2, 2, [b2[1], b2[2]])

            if int_p == 0:
                source_angle_1 = angle(location_end1, b2[1])
                source_angle_2 = angle(location_end2, b2[1])
            else:
                int_p = int_p[0]
                source_angle_1 = angle(location_end1, b2[1])
                source_angle_2 = angle(int_p, b2[1])

        # endpoint 2 on the correct side
        elif sign_pseudo * sign_end1 <= 0 and sign_pseudo * sign_end2 > 0:

            # there should be one intersection point
            int_p = line_arc_intersection(steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2, 2, [b2[1], b2[2]])

            if int_p == 0:
                source_angle_1 = angle(location_end1, b2[1])
                source_angle_2 = angle(location_end2, b2[1])
            else:
                int_p = int_p[0]
                source_angle_1 = angle(int_p, b2[1])
                source_angle_2 = angle(location_end2, b2[1])

        # both endpoints on the correct side
        else:
            intersections = line_arc_intersection(steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2, 2, [b2[1], b2[2]])

            # no intersections
            if intersections == 0:
                source_angle_1 = angle(location_end1, b2[1])
                source_angle_2 = angle(location_end2, b2[1])

            # one intersection (tangent)
            elif len(intersections) == 1:

                v2 = eqpoint(steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2)
                v3 = [steiner_location_2[i] + (v2[i] - steiner_location_2[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
                v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (steiner_location_1[i] - steiner_location_2[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

                top_int = line_arc_intersection(v3, v1, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])

                if top_int != 0:
                    relevant_angle = angle(top_int, b2[1])
                else:
                    left_int = line_arc_intersection(v3, v2, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])
                    if left_int != 0:
                        relevant_angle = angle(left_int, b2[1])
                    else:
                        right_int = line_arc_intersection(v1, v2, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])
                        if right_int != 0:
                            relevant_angle = angle(right_int, b2[1])
                        else:
                            return 0

                tangent_intersection = intersections[0]
                tangent_angle = angle(tangent_intersection, b2[1])

                if tangent_angle <= relevant_angle <= b2[2][4]:
                    source_angle_1 = tangent_angle
                    source_angle_2 = b2[2][4]
                elif tangent_angle > b2[2][4] and (relevant_angle >= tangent_angle or relevant_angle <= b2[2][4]):
                    source_angle_1 = tangent_angle
                    source_angle_2 = b2[2][4]
                else:
                    source_angle_1 = b2[2][3]
                    source_angle_2 = tangent_angle

            # two intersections
            else:
                angle_p = angle(intersections[0], b2[1])
                angle_q = angle(intersections[1], b2[1])

                if b2[2][4] >= b2[2][3]:
                    larger_int_angle = max(angle_p, angle_q)
                    smaller_int_angle = min(angle_p, angle_q)

                else:
                    if angle_p <= b2[2][4] and angle_q >= b2[2][3]:
                        smaller_int_angle = angle_q
                        larger_int_angle = angle_p
                    elif angle_q <= b2[2][4] and angle_p >= b2[2][3]:
                        smaller_int_angle = angle_p
                        larger_int_angle = angle_q
                    else:
                        larger_int_angle = max(angle_p, angle_q)
                        smaller_int_angle = min(angle_p, angle_q)

                v2 = eqpoint(steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2)
                v3 = [steiner_location_2[i] + (v2[i] - steiner_location_2[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
                v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (steiner_location_1[i] - steiner_location_2[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

                top_int = line_arc_intersection(v3, v1, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])

                if top_int != 0:
                    relevant_angle = angle(top_int, b2[1])
                else:
                    left_int = line_arc_intersection(v3, v2, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])
                    if left_int != 0:
                        relevant_angle = angle(left_int, b2[1])
                    else:
                        right_int = line_arc_intersection(v1, v2, 0, [b2[1], b2[2]])
                        if right_int != 0:
                            relevant_angle = angle(right_int, b2[1])
                        else:
                            return 0

                if larger_int_angle <= relevant_angle <= b2[2][4]:
                    source_angle_1 = larger_int_angle
                    source_angle_2 = b2[2][4]
                elif larger_int_angle > b2[2][4] and (relevant_angle >= larger_int_angle or relevant_angle <= b2[2][4]):
                    source_angle_1 = larger_int_angle
                    source_angle_2 = b2[2][4]
                else:
                    source_angle_1 = b2[2][3]
                    source_angle_2 = smaller_int_angle

    cone = cone_intersection(source_angle_1, source_angle_2, angle_1, angle_2)

    if cone == 0:
        return 0
    else:
        [final_1, final_2] = cone

    final_1_projection = [b2[1][0] + math.cos(final_1), b2[1][1] + math.sin(final_1)]
    final_2_projection = [b2[1][0] + math.cos(final_2), b2[1][1] + math.sin(final_2)]

    int_1 = line_intersection([steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2], [b2[1], final_1_projection], 2, 1)
    int_2 = line_intersection([steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2], [b2[1], final_2_projection], 2, 1)

    return [[steiner_location_1, steiner_location_2], [int_1[0], int_1[1], int_2[0], int_2[1]]]


def back_projection(new_r, new_l, full_source_arcseg):
    source_pseudo = full_source_arcseg[0]
    source_arcseg = full_source_arcseg[1]

    eps = 0.000001

    # source arc
    if len(source_arcseg) == 7:
        if source_arcseg[2] != 0:

            temp_pseudo_angle1 = angle(new_r, source_pseudo)
            temp_pseudo_angle2 = angle(new_l, source_pseudo)

            r2 = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[5]),
                  source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[5])]
            l2 = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[6]),
                  source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[6])]

            angle_r2 = angle(r2, source_pseudo)
            angle_l2 = angle(l2, source_pseudo)

            if abs(temp_pseudo_angle1 - angle_r2) > eps:

                if (dist(new_r, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]]) - source_arcseg[2]) > eps:
                    new_r2 = line_arc_intersection(source_pseudo, new_r, 0, full_source_arcseg)
                    temp_centre_angle1 = angle(new_r2, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]])
                else:
                    temp_centre_angle1 = angle(new_r, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]])

            else:
                temp_centre_angle1 = source_arcseg[5]

            if abs(temp_pseudo_angle2 - angle_l2) > eps:

                if (dist(new_l, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]]) - source_arcseg[2]) > eps:
                    new_l2 = line_arc_intersection(source_pseudo, new_l, 0, full_source_arcseg)
                    temp_centre_angle2 = angle(new_l2, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]])
                else:
                    temp_centre_angle2 = angle(new_l, [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]])
            else:
                temp_centre_angle2 = source_arcseg[6]

            return [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1], source_arcseg[2], temp_pseudo_angle1, temp_pseudo_angle2,
                    temp_centre_angle1, temp_centre_angle2]

        else:
            return source_arcseg

    # source segment
    else:
        new_r2 = line_intersection([source_pseudo, new_r],
                                   [[source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]], [source_arcseg[2], source_arcseg[3]]], 0, 0)
        new_l2 = line_intersection([source_pseudo, new_l],
                                   [[source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]], [source_arcseg[2], source_arcseg[3]]], 0, 0)

        print(f"newr: {new_r}")
        print(f"newl: {new_l}")

        print(f"source seg: {source_arcseg}")
        print(f"source pseudo: {source_pseudo}")

        return [new_r2[0], new_r2[1], new_l2[0], new_l2[1]]


def triple_merge(b1, b2, b3):
    if wedge_on == True and len(b1[3]) == len(b3[3]) == 1:
        right_check = find(hull_vertices, b1[3][0])
        if right_check != []:
            left_check = (right_check[0] + 1) % len(hull_vertices)
            if hull_vertices[left_check] == b3[3][0]:
                return 0

    if adj_deg4_on == True:
        if len(b1[2]) == 8 or len(b2[2]) == 8 or len(b3[2]) == 8:
            return 0

    new_segment = triple_projection(b1, b2, b3)
    if new_segment == 0:
        return 0

    b1vertices = copy.deepcopy(b1[0])
    b2vertices = copy.deepcopy(b2[0])
    b3vertices = copy.deepcopy(b3[0])

    for i in range(len(b2vertices) - 1):
        b2vertices[i][3] += len(b1vertices)

    for i in range(len(b3vertices) - 1):
        b3vertices[i][3] += len(b1vertices) + len(b2vertices)

    b1vertices[-1][3] = len(b1vertices) + len(b2vertices) + len(b3vertices)
    b2vertices[-1][3] = len(b1vertices) + len(b2vertices) + len(b3vertices)
    b3vertices[-1][3] = len(b1vertices) + len(b2vertices) + len(b3vertices)

    new_vertex_list = b1vertices + b2vertices + b3vertices + [[b2[1], new_segment[1], -2, -1]]

    new_pseudo = b2[1]

    new_terminal_list = list(set(b1[3]) | set(b2[3]) | set(b3[3]))

    partial_length = dist(b1[1], b3[1])

    steiner_segment = new_segment[1]
    first_steiner = new_segment[0][0]
    third_steiner = new_segment[0][1]

    # temp_source_arcseg = back_projection([steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]], [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]], [b2[1], b2[2]])

    if alpha_on == True:
        result = alpha_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]],
                            [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]], [b2[1], b2[2]])
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            new_r, new_l = result
            steiner_segment = new_r + new_l

    # temp_source_arcseg = back_projection([steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]], [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]], [b2[1], b2[2]])

    if rhombus_on == True:
        result = rhombus_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]],
                              [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]], [b2[1], b2[2]])
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            new_r, new_l = result
            steiner_segment = new_r + new_l

    if alpha_rhombus_on == True:
        result = alpha_rhombus_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]],
                                    [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]], [b2[1], b2[2]])
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            new_r, new_l = result
            steiner_segment = new_r + new_l

    if second_ar_on == True:
        result = triple_second_ar_test(steiner_segment, b1, b2, b3)
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            steiner_segment = result

    if vertical_lunes_on == True:
        result = vertical_lunes_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]],
                                     [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]])
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            new_r, new_l = result
            steiner_segment = new_r + new_l

    if triple_bottle_on == True:
        result = triple_bottleneck_test(b1[3], b2[3], b3[3], first_steiner, third_steiner, steiner_segment)
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            new_r, new_l = result
            steiner_segment = new_r + new_l

    if source_angle_on == True:
        result = source_angle_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, steiner_segment, b2[1])
        if result == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            steiner_segment = result

    steiner_segment = steiner_segment + first_steiner + third_steiner

    return [new_vertex_list, new_pseudo, steiner_segment, new_terminal_list, b1[4] + b2[4] + b3[4] + partial_length]


def alpha_test(s1, s3, r, l, full_source_arcseg):
    p2 = full_source_arcseg[0]
    source_arcseg = full_source_arcseg[1]

    v2 = eqpoint(s1, s3)
    v3 = [s3[i] + (v2[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
    v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (s1[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

    # plotting the alpha line
    # plt.plot([v1[0], v3[0]], [v1[1], v3[1]], 'b')

    # if source interval is an arc
    if len(source_arcseg) == 7:

        vector_alpha = [v1[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        vector_v3_s3 = [s3[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        side_s3 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_s3)

        # if the source pseudoterminal is level 0 (terminal)
        if source_arcseg[2] == 0:
            vector_v3_p2 = [p2[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]

            side_p2 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_p2)

            # source terminal is within alpha
            if side_s3 * side_p2 > 0:
                return 0
            else:
                return r, l

        r2 = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[5]),
              source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[5])]
        l2 = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[6]),
              source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[6])]

        vector_v3_r2 = [r2[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        vector_v3_l2 = [l2[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]

        side_r2 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_r2)
        side_l2 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_l2)

        # both l2 and r2 lie within the alpha line
        if side_s3 * side_l2 > 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 > 0:
            return 0

        # only r2 lies within the alpha line
        elif side_s3 * side_l2 <= 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 > 0:

            q = line_arc_intersection(v3, v1, 2, full_source_arcseg)[0]

            alpha_angle1 = angle(q, p2)
            alpha_angle2 = angle(l2, p2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(angle(r, p2), angle(l, p2), alpha_angle1, alpha_angle2)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

            new_r = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l

        # only l2 lies within the alpha line
        elif side_s3 * side_l2 > 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 <= 0:

            q = line_arc_intersection(v3, v1, 2, full_source_arcseg)[0]

            alpha_angle1 = angle(r2, p2)
            alpha_angle2 = angle(q, p2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(angle(r, p2), angle(l, p2), alpha_angle1, alpha_angle2)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0
            new_r = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l

        # both l2 and r2 lie beyond the alpha line
        else:
            return r, l

    # source interval is a segment
    else:
        vector_alpha = [v1[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        vector_v3_s3 = [s3[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        side_s3 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_s3)

        r2 = [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]]
        l2 = [source_arcseg[2], source_arcseg[3]]

        vector_v3_r2 = [r2[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
        vector_v3_l2 = [l2[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]

        side_r2 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_r2)
        side_l2 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_l2)

        # both l2 and r2 lie within the alpha line
        if side_s3 * side_l2 > 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 > 0:
            return 0

        # only r2 lies within the alpha line
        elif side_s3 * side_l2 <= 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 > 0:
            q = line_intersection([r2, l2], [v1, v3], 0, 2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(angle(r, p2), angle(l, p2), angle(q, p2), angle(l2, p2))

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

            new_r = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l

        # only l2 lies within the alpha line
        elif side_s3 * side_l2 > 0 and side_s3 * side_r2 <= 0:
            q = line_intersection([r2, l2], [v1, v3], 0, 2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(angle(r, p2), angle(l, p2), angle(r2, p2), angle(q, p2))

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

            new_r = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection([p2, [p2[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), p2[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l

        # both l2 and r2 lie beyond the alpha line
        else:
            return r, l


def rhombus_test(s1, s3, r, l, full_source_arcseg):
    eq = eqpoint(s1, s3)

    source_pseudo = full_source_arcseg[0]
    source_arcseg = full_source_arcseg[1]

    # plotting the rhombus lines
    # plt.plot([s1[0], eq[0]], [s1[1], eq[1]], 'b')
    # plt.plot([s3[0], eq[0]], [s3[1], eq[1]], 'b')

    current_angle1 = angle(r, source_pseudo)
    current_angle2 = angle(l, source_pseudo)

    # source interval is a segment
    if len(source_arcseg) == 8:
        source_r = [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]]
        source_l = [source_arcseg[2], source_arcseg[3]]

        r_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_r, s1, s3, eq)
        l_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_l, s1, s3, eq)

        # both endpoints lie inside the triangle
        if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == True:
            return r, l

        # r is inside, l is outside
        elif r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == False:

            p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s3, eq], 0, 0)
            if p == 0:
                p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, eq], 0, 0)
                if p == 0:
                    p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, s3], 0, 0)

            intersection_angle = angle(p, source_pseudo)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, angle(source_r, source_pseudo),
                                        intersection_angle)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        # r is outside, l is inside
        elif r_in_triangle == False and l_in_triangle == True:

            p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, eq], 0, 0)
            if p == 0:
                p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s3, eq], 0, 0)
                if p == 0:
                    p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, s3], 0, 0)

            intersection_angle = angle(p, source_pseudo)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, intersection_angle,
                                        angle(source_l, source_pseudo))

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        # both endpoints are outside the triangle
        else:
            u = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, eq], 0, 0)
            v = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s3, eq], 0, 0)
            w = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [s1, s3], 0, 0)

            # no intersections with the triangle
            if u == v == w == 0:
                return 0

            if (u == 0 and v == 0) or (u == 0 and w == 0) or (v == 0 and w == 0):
                return 0

            if u == 0:
                angle1 = angle(v, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(w, source_pseudo)

            elif v == 0:
                angle1 = angle(u, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(w, source_pseudo)

            # w == 0
            else:
                angle1 = angle(u, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(v, source_pseudo)

            angle_source_r = angle(source_r, source_pseudo)
            angle_source_l = angle(source_l, source_pseudo)

            if angle_source_l >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = min(angle1, angle2)
                rhombus_angle2 = max(angle1, angle2)

            elif angle1 <= angle_source_l and angle2 >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = angle2
                rhombus_angle2 = angle1

            elif angle2 <= angle_source_l and angle1 >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = angle1
                rhombus_angle2 = angle2

            else:
                rhombus_angle1 = min(angle1, angle2)
                rhombus_angle2 = max(angle1, angle2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, rhombus_angle1, rhombus_angle2)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        new_r = line_intersection(
            [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l],
            1, 2)
        new_l = line_intersection(
            [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l],
            1, 2)

        return new_r, new_l

    # source interval is an arc
    else:
        # source pseudoterminal is a terminal
        if source_arcseg[2] == 0:
            if point_in_triangle(source_pseudo, s1, s3, eq):
                return r, l
            else:
                return 0

        # source pseudoterminal is not a terminal
        else:
            source_r = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[5]),
                        source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[5])]
            source_l = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[6]),
                        source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[6])]

            r_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_r, s1, s3, eq)
            l_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_l, s1, s3, eq)

            # both endpoints lie inside the triangle
            if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == True:
                return r, l

            intersection_points = []

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(s3, eq, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(s1, eq, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(s1, s3, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            # at least one endpoint is outside and there are no intersections, so the entire
            # arc lies outside the triangle
            if intersection_points == []:
                return 0

            intersection_angles = []
            for int_point in intersection_points:
                intersection_angles.append(angle(int_point, source_pseudo))

            # r is inside, l is outside
            if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == False:

                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, source_arcseg[3], new_l_angle)

                else:
                    for int_angle in intersection_angles:
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            int_angle += 2 * np.pi

                        new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)
                        new_l_angle = new_l_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                        overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, source_arcseg[3], new_l_angle)


            # r is outside, l is inside
            elif r_in_triangle == False and l_in_triangle == True:

                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, source_arcseg[4])

                else:
                    for int_angle in intersection_angles:
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            int_angle += 2 * np.pi

                        new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                        new_r_angle = new_r_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                        overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, source_arcseg[4])

            # both endpoints are outside the triangle
            else:
                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, new_l_angle)

                else:
                    for int_index, int_angle in enumerate(intersection_angles):
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            intersection_angles[int_index] += 2 * np.pi

                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                    new_r_angle = new_r_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)
                    new_l_angle = new_l_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, new_l_angle)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

            new_r = line_intersection(
                [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]],
                [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection(
                [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]],
                [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l


def alpha_rhombus_test(s1, s3, r, l, full_source_arcseg):
    source_pseudo = full_source_arcseg[0]
    source_arcseg = full_source_arcseg[1]

    v2 = eqpoint(s1, s3)
    v3 = [s3[i] + (v2[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
    v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (s1[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

    # plotting the alpha+rhombus lines
    # plt.plot([v1[0], v2[0]], [v1[1], v2[1]], 'b')
    # plt.plot([v1[0], v3[0]], [v1[1], v3[1]], 'b')
    # plt.plot([v2[0], v3[0]], [v2[1], v3[1]], 'b')

    current_angle1 = angle(r, source_pseudo)
    current_angle2 = angle(l, source_pseudo)

    # source interval is a segment
    if len(source_arcseg) == 8:
        source_r = [source_arcseg[0], source_arcseg[1]]
        source_l = [source_arcseg[2], source_arcseg[3]]

        r_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_r, v1, v2, v3)
        l_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_l, v1, v2, v3)

        # both endpoints lie inside the triangle
        if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == True:
            return r, l

        # r is inside, l is outside
        elif r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == False:

            p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v2], 0, 0)
            if p == 0:
                p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v3], 0, 0)
                if p == 0:
                    p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v2, v3], 0, 0)

            intersection_angle = angle(p, source_pseudo)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, angle(source_r, source_pseudo),
                                        intersection_angle)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        # r is outside, l is inside
        elif r_in_triangle == False and l_in_triangle == True:

            p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v2], 0, 0)
            if p == 0:
                p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v3], 0, 0)
                if p == 0:
                    p = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v2, v3], 0, 0)

            intersection_angle = angle(p, source_pseudo)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, intersection_angle,
                                        angle(source_l, source_pseudo))

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        # both endpoints are outside the triangle
        else:
            u = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v2], 0, 0)
            v = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v1, v3], 0, 0)
            w = line_intersection([source_r, source_l], [v2, v3], 0, 0)

            # no intersections with the triangle
            if u == v == w == 0:
                return 0

            if u == 0:
                if v == 0 or w == 0:
                    return 0
                angle1 = angle(v, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(w, source_pseudo)

            elif v == 0:
                if u == 0 or w == 0:
                    return 0
                angle1 = angle(u, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(w, source_pseudo)

            # w == 0
            else:
                if u == 0 or v == 0:
                    return 0
                angle1 = angle(u, source_pseudo)
                angle2 = angle(v, source_pseudo)

            angle_source_r = angle(source_r, source_pseudo)
            angle_source_l = angle(source_l, source_pseudo)

            if angle_source_l >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = min(angle1, angle2)
                rhombus_angle2 = max(angle1, angle2)

            elif angle1 <= angle_source_l and angle2 >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = angle2
                rhombus_angle2 = angle1

            elif angle2 <= angle_source_l and angle1 >= angle_source_r:

                rhombus_angle1 = angle1
                rhombus_angle2 = angle2

            else:
                rhombus_angle1 = min(angle1, angle2)
                rhombus_angle2 = max(angle1, angle2)

            overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, rhombus_angle1, rhombus_angle2)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

        new_r = line_intersection(
            [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]], [r, l],
            1, 2)
        new_l = line_intersection(
            [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]], [r, l],
            1, 2)

        return new_r, new_l

    # source interval is an arc
    else:
        # source pseudoterminal is a terminal
        if source_arcseg[2] == 0:
            if point_in_triangle(source_pseudo, v1, v2, v3):
                return r, l
            else:
                return 0

        # source pseudoterminal is not a terminal
        else:
            source_arc_r = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[5]),
                            source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[5])]
            source_arc_l = [source_arcseg[0] + source_arcseg[2] * math.cos(source_arcseg[6]),
                            source_arcseg[1] + source_arcseg[2] * math.sin(source_arcseg[6])]

            r_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_arc_r, v1, v2, v3)
            l_in_triangle = point_in_triangle(source_arc_l, v1, v2, v3)

            # both endpoints lie inside the triangle
            if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == True:
                return r, l

            intersection_points = []

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(v1, v2, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(v1, v3, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            temp_ints = line_arc_intersection(v2, v3, 0.5, full_source_arcseg)

            if temp_ints != 0:
                for int in temp_ints:
                    intersection_points.append(int)

            # at least one endpoint is outside and there are no intersections, so the entire
            # arc lies outside the triangle
            if intersection_points == []:
                return 0

            intersection_angles = []
            for int_point in intersection_points:
                intersection_angles.append(angle(int_point, source_pseudo))

            # r is inside, l is outside
            if r_in_triangle == True and l_in_triangle == False:

                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, source_arcseg[3], new_l_angle)

                else:
                    for int_angle in intersection_angles:
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            int_angle += 2 * np.pi

                        new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)
                        new_l_angle = new_l_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                        overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, source_arcseg[3], new_l_angle)


            # r is outside, l is inside
            elif r_in_triangle == False and l_in_triangle == True:

                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, source_arcseg[4])

                else:
                    for int_angle in intersection_angles:
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            int_angle += 2 * np.pi

                        new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                        new_r_angle = new_r_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                        overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, source_arcseg[4])

            # both endpoints are outside the triangle
            else:
                if source_arcseg[4] >= source_arcseg[3]:
                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, new_l_angle)

                else:
                    for int_index, int_angle in enumerate(intersection_angles):
                        if int_angle <= source_arcseg[4]:
                            intersection_angles[int_index] += 2 * np.pi

                    new_r_angle = min(intersection_angles)
                    new_r_angle = new_r_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                    new_l_angle = max(intersection_angles)
                    new_l_angle = new_l_angle % (2 * np.pi)

                    overlap = cone_intersection(current_angle1, current_angle2, new_r_angle, new_l_angle)

            if overlap == 0:
                return 0

            new_r = line_intersection(
                [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[0]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[0])]],
                [r, l], 1, 2)
            new_l = line_intersection(
                [source_pseudo, [source_pseudo[0] + math.cos(overlap[1]), source_pseudo[1] + math.sin(overlap[1])]],
                [r, l], 1, 2)

            return new_r, new_l


def triple_second_ar_test(steiner_segment, b1, b2, b3):
    # no branch was previous triple-merged, then the test is redundant
    if len(b1[2]) == 7 and len(b2[2]) == 7 and len(b3[2]) == 7:
        return steiner_segment

    if second_ar_on == True and ((alpha_on == True and rhombus_on == True) or alpha_rhombus_on == True):

        branches = [b1, b2, b3]
        new_pseudo = b2[1]

        for branch in branches:

            # branch was previously triple-merged
            if len(branch[2]) == 8:
                s_r = [branch[2][4], branch[2][5]]
                s_l = [branch[2][6], branch[2][7]]

                upper_eq = eqpoint(s_l, s_r)

                v_l = [s_l[i] + (upper_eq[i] - s_l[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
                v_r = [s_r[i] + (upper_eq[i] - s_r[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

                # plotting the alpha+rhombus lines
                # plt.plot([v_l[0], v_r[0]], [v_l[1], v_r[1]], 'b')
                # plt.plot([v_l[0], upper_eq[0]], [v_l[1], upper_eq[1]], 'b')
                # plt.plot([upper_eq[0], v_r[0]], [upper_eq[1], v_r[1]], 'b')

                right_endpoint = [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]]
                left_endpoint = [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]]

                right_inside = point_in_triangle(right_endpoint, v_l, v_r, upper_eq)
                left_inside = point_in_triangle(left_endpoint, v_l, v_r, upper_eq)

                if right_inside == True and left_inside == True:
                    continue

                # at least one endpoint is outside the triangle
                intersect_pts = []

                first_side = line_intersection([v_l, v_r], [right_endpoint, left_endpoint], 0, 0)
                if first_side != 0:
                    intersect_pts.append(first_side)

                second_side = line_intersection([v_l, upper_eq], [right_endpoint, left_endpoint], 0, 0)
                if second_side != 0:
                    intersect_pts.append(second_side)

                third_side = line_intersection([upper_eq, v_r], [right_endpoint, left_endpoint], 0, 0)
                if third_side != 0:
                    intersect_pts.append(third_side)

                if left_inside == False and right_inside == False and len(intersect_pts) == 0:
                    return 0

                if left_inside == False and right_inside == False:
                    if dist(intersect_pts[0], [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]]) < dist(intersect_pts[1],
                                                                                               [steiner_segment[0],
                                                                                                steiner_segment[1]]):
                        steiner_segment = [intersect_pts[0][0], intersect_pts[0][1], intersect_pts[1][0],
                                           intersect_pts[1][1]]
                elif left_inside == False and right_inside == True:
                    steiner_segment = [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1], intersect_pts[0][0], intersect_pts[0][1]]
                elif left_inside == True and right_inside == False:
                    steiner_segment = [intersect_pts[0][0], intersect_pts[0][1], steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]]

        return steiner_segment


def no_terminals_inside_lune(u, v):
    # returns 1 if there are no terminals inside L(u, v)
    # otherwise returns a list of indices of terminals that are inside L(u, v)

    eps = 0.0000001

    lune_length = dist(u, v)

    lst = []

    for index, terminal in enumerate(N):
        if dist(terminal, u) < lune_length - eps and dist(terminal, v) < lune_length - eps:
            lst.append(index)

    if len(lst) == 0:
        return 1
    else:
        return lst


def vertical_lunes_test(s1, s3, r, l):
    steinerseg_length = dist(s1, s3)

    if no_terminals_inside_lune(s1, r) != 1:
        return 0

    lune_result1 = no_terminals_inside_lune(s1, l)

    if lune_result1 != 1:

        dist_lst = []
        for terminal_index in lune_result1:

            terminal = N[terminal_index]
            theta = angle_three(terminal, s1, s3)

            if theta > math.pi / 3:
                dist_lst.append(dist(terminal, s1) / (2 * math.cos(theta)))
            else:
                dist_lst.append(dist(terminal, s1))

            min_dist = min(dist_lst)

            l_prime = [s1[0] + min_dist * (s3[0] - s1[0]) / steinerseg_length,
                       s1[1] + min_dist * (s3[1] - s1[1]) / steinerseg_length]
    else:
        l_prime = l.copy()

    if no_terminals_inside_lune(s3, l_prime) != 1:
        return 0

    lune_result2 = no_terminals_inside_lune(s3, r)

    if lune_result2 != 1:

        dist_lst = []
        for terminal_index in lune_result2:

            terminal = N[terminal_index]
            theta = angle_three(terminal, s3, s1)

            if theta > math.pi / 3:
                dist_lst.append(dist(terminal, s3) / (2 * math.cos(theta)))
            else:
                dist_lst.append(dist(terminal, s3))

            min_dist = min(dist_lst)

            r_prime = [s3[0] + min_dist * (s1[0] - s3[0]) / steinerseg_length,
                       s3[1] + min_dist * (s1[1] - s3[1]) / steinerseg_length]
    else:
        r_prime = r.copy()

    return r_prime, l_prime


def bottleneck_between_sets(set_1, set_2):
    min_bottleneck = 9999999

    for terminal_1 in set_1:
        for terminal_2 in set_2:
            current_bottleneck = bottleneck_bound_array[terminal_1][terminal_2]

            if current_bottleneck < min_bottleneck:
                min_bottleneck = current_bottleneck

    return min_bottleneck


def triple_bottleneck_test(b1_terminals, b2_terminals, b3_terminals, first_steiner, third_steiner, steiner_seg):
    # input: result = triple_bottleneck_test(b1[3], b2[3], b3[3], first_steiner, third_steiner, steiner_segment)

    N_12 = b1_terminals + b2_terminals
    # N_13 = b1_terminals + b3_terminals
    N_23 = b2_terminals + b3_terminals

    r_A = [steiner_seg[0], steiner_seg[1]]
    l_A = [steiner_seg[2], steiner_seg[3]]

    bound_ss1 = bottleneck_between_sets(b1_terminals, N_23)

    if dist(first_steiner, r_A) > bound_ss1:
        return 0

    if dist(first_steiner, l_A) > bound_ss1:
        # need to create a new l_A
        vector_s1s3 = [third_steiner[x] - first_steiner[x] for x in range(2)]
        magnitude_s1s3 = math.sqrt(vector_s1s3[0] ** 2 + vector_s1s3[1] ** 2)

        l_A = [first_steiner[x] + vector_s1s3[x] * bound_ss1 / magnitude_s1s3 for x in range(2)]

    bound_ss3 = bottleneck_between_sets(b3_terminals, N_12)

    if dist(third_steiner, l_A) > bound_ss3:
        return 0

    if dist(third_steiner, r_A) > bound_ss3:
        # need to create a new r_A
        vector_s3s1 = [first_steiner[x] - third_steiner[x] for x in range(2)]
        magnitude_s3s1 = math.sqrt(vector_s3s1[0] ** 2 + vector_s3s1[1] ** 2)

        r_A = [third_steiner[x] + vector_s3s1[x] * bound_ss3 / magnitude_s3s1 for x in range(2)]

    # check that l_A and r_A are in the correct positions
    if dist(first_steiner, l_A) < dist(first_steiner, r_A) or dist(third_steiner, r_A) < dist(third_steiner, l_A):
        return 0

    return r_A, l_A


def source_angle_test(first_steiner, third_steiner, steiner_segment, p2):
    current_r = [steiner_segment[0], steiner_segment[1]]
    current_l = [steiner_segment[2], steiner_segment[3]]

    if angle_three(p2, current_l, current_r) < math.pi / 3:

        rotated_point = rotate(first_steiner, third_steiner, 5 * math.pi / 3)
        parallel_vector = [rotated_point[x] - first_steiner[x] for x in range(2)]
        other_endpoint = [p2[x] + parallel_vector[x] for x in range(2)]

        l_prime = line_intersection([first_steiner, third_steiner], [p2, other_endpoint], 2, 2)

        # if l' does not lie within the interval]
        if dist(l_prime, current_l) > dist(current_r, current_l):
            return 0

        current_l = l_prime

    if angle_three(p2, current_r, current_l) < math.pi / 3:

        rotated_point = rotate(third_steiner, first_steiner, math.pi / 3)
        parallel_vector = [rotated_point[x] - third_steiner[x] for x in range(2)]
        other_endpoint = [p2[x] + parallel_vector[x] for x in range(2)]

        r_prime = line_intersection([first_steiner, third_steiner], [p2, other_endpoint], 2, 2)

        # if r' does not lie within the interval
        if dist(r_prime, current_r) > dist(current_l, current_r):
            return 0

        current_r = r_prime

    steiner_segment = current_r + current_l

    return steiner_segment


def triple_threelune_test(c_l, c_r):
    return


def triple_fourlune_test(c_l, c_r):
    return


def termination_check(b1, terminal):
    # returns 1 if b1 can be terminated with terminal
    # returns 0 otherwise

    # arc
    if len(b1[2]) == 7:

        centre = [b1[2][0], b1[2][1]]

        if dist(centre, terminal) <= b1[2][2]:
            return 0

        terminal_angle = angle(terminal, b1[1])

        if b1[2][4] >= terminal_angle >= b1[2][3]:
            return 1

        elif b1[2][3] > b1[2][4] and (terminal_angle <= b1[2][4] or terminal_angle >= b1[2][3]):
            return 1

        else:
            return 0

    # segment
    else:
        soln = line_intersection([b1[1], terminal], [[b1[2][0], b1[2][1]], [b1[2][2], b1[2][3]]], 0, 0)

        if soln == 0:
            return 0
        else:
            if (alpha_on == True and rhombus_on == True) or alpha_rhombus_on == True:

                s1 = [b1[2][4], b1[2][5]]
                s3 = [b1[2][6], b1[2][7]]

                eq = eqpoint(s3, s1)

                v3 = [s3[i] + (eq[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
                v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (s1[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

                if point_in_triangle(terminal, v1, v3, eq) == True:
                    return 1
                else:
                    return 0


            elif rhombus_on == True:

                s1 = [b1[2][4], b1[2][5]]
                s3 = [b1[2][6], b1[2][7]]

                eq = eqpoint(s3, s1)

                if point_in_triangle(terminal, s1, s3, eq) == True:
                    return 1
                else:
                    return 0


            elif alpha_on == True:

                s1 = [b1[2][4], b1[2][5]]
                s3 = [b1[2][6], b1[2][7]]

                eq = eqpoint(s3, s1)

                v3 = [s3[i] + (eq[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]
                v1 = [v3[i] + 2 * (s1[i] - s3[i]) / 3 for i in range(2)]

                vector_alpha = [v1[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]

                vector_v3_s3 = [s3[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
                side_s3 = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_s3)

                vector_v3_terminal = [terminal[i] - v3[i] for i in range(2)]
                side_terminal = np.cross(vector_alpha, vector_v3_terminal)

                if side_s3 * side_terminal <= 0:
                    return 1
                else:
                    return 0


def termination_merge(b1, N, t):
    # b1 is the branch, t is the index of the terminal, N is the list of terminals
    terminal = N[t]
    result = termination_check(b1, terminal)

    # cannot be terminated
    if result == 0:
        return 0

    else:
        b1vertices = copy.deepcopy(b1[0])
        b1vertices[-1][3] = len(b1vertices)

        FST_vertices = b1vertices + [[terminal[0], terminal[1], t, -1]]
        # no pseudoterminal
        pseudo = 0
        # no arc/segment
        arcseg = 0
        new_terminal_list = list(set(b1[3]) | {t})

        length = dist(b1[1], terminal)

        FST = [FST_vertices, pseudo, arcseg, new_terminal_list, b1[4] + length]

    return FST


"""
def cut_constraint(model, where):

    # check if the LP relaxation at this branch-and-bound node has an integer solution
    if where == GRB.Callback.MIPSOL:

        # retrieve the LP relaxation solution at the B&B node
        xval = model.cbGetSolution(model._x)

        # which FSTs are selected?
        selected_fsts = [f for f in model._FST_no if xval[f] > 0.5]

        # for each component, add a cut-constraint
        for component in nx.connected_components(model._FST_)
