def post_lune_test(F):
    # removes FSTs from F that violate the lune test

    for F_index, F_i in enumerate(F):

        fst_remain_indices = [i for i in range(len(F_i))]

        for fst_index, fst in enumerate(F_i):

            vertexlist = fst[0]
            terminal_indices = [i for i in range(len(N))]
            n_fst = len(vertexlist)
            eps = 0.000001

            fst_gone = 0

            for edge_index in range(n_fst - 1):
                u = vertexlist[edge_index]
                v = vertexlist[u[3]]

                endpoints = [u[2], v[2]]
                non_endpoint_terminals = [x for x in terminal_indices if x not in endpoints]

                uv_length = dist([u[0], u[1]], [v[0], v[1]])

                for term_index in non_endpoint_terminals:
                    terminal = N[term_index]
                    if dist([u[0], u[1]], terminal) < uv_length - eps:
                        if dist([v[0], v[1]], terminal) < uv_length - eps:
                            fst_remain_indices.remove(fst_index)
                            fst_gone = 1
                            break

                if fst_gone == 1:
                    break

                if fst_gone == 0:
                    for vertex_index in [x for x in range(n_fst) if x != edge_index and x != u[3]]:
                        if vertexlist[vertex_index][2] < 0:
                            if dist([u[0], u[1]],
                                    [vertexlist[vertex_index][0], vertexlist[vertex_index][1]]) < uv_length - eps:
                                if dist([v[0], v[1]],
                                        [vertexlist[vertex_index][0], vertexlist[vertex_index][1]]) < uv_length - eps:
                                    fst_remain_indices.remove(fst_index)
                                    fst_gone = 1
                                    break

                if fst_gone == 1:
                    break

        F_i_replacement = [F_i[x] for x in fst_remain_indices]

        F[F_index] = F_i_replacement.copy()


def post_bottle_test(F):
    # removes FSTs from F that violate the bottleneck test
    for F_index, F_i in enumerate(F):

        # no need to check FSTs with no steiner points (MST edges)
        if F_index == 0:
            continue

        fst_remain_indices = [i for i in range(len(F_i))]

        for fst_index, fst in enumerate(F_i):

            fst_vertices = fst[0]
            fst_gone = 0

            for edge_index in range(len(fst_vertices) - 1):

                if fst_gone == 1:
                    break

                ancestors_found = 0
                ancestors = [edge_index]

                while ancestors_found == 0:

                    ancestors_found = 1

                    for vertex_index, vertex in enumerate(fst_vertices[:len(fst_vertices) - 1]):
                        if vertex[3] in ancestors and vertex_index not in ancestors:
                            ancestors.append(vertex_index)
                            ancestors_found = 0

                ancestors = [fst_vertices[x][2] for x in ancestors if fst_vertices[x][2] > -0.5]

                complement = [x for x in fst[3] if x not in ancestors]

                bbound = bottleneck_between_sets(ancestors, complement)

                u = fst_vertices[edge_index]
                v = fst_vertices[u[3]]

                if dist(u, v) > bbound:
                    fst_remain_indices.remove(fst_index)
                    fst_gone = 1

        F_i_replacement = [F_i[x] for x in fst_remain_indices]

        F[F_index] = F_i_replacement.copy()


def concatenation_lazy(FST_set, terminal_no, max_steiner_no):
    concat_start = time.perf_counter()

    FST_no = 0

    for i in range(max_steiner_no + 1):
        FST_no += len(FST_set[i])

    print(f"total # of FSTs is {FST_no}")
    # number of Steiner points in each hyperedge
    S = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]
    # length of each hyperedge
    L = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]
    # terminals in each hyperedge
    T = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]

    FST_index = 0
    for i in range(max_steiner_no + 1):
        for fst in FST_set[i]:
            S[FST_index] = len(fst[0]) - len(fst[3])
            L[FST_index] = fst[4]
            T[FST_index] = fst[3]
            FST_index += 1

    # Define callback function for lazy constraints
    def lazy_callback(model, where):

        if where == GRB.Callback.MIPSOL:

            # Retrieve current solution
            x_values = model.cbGetSolution(model._vars)

            xvalues = [x for x in model._vars if x_values[x] > 0.5]

            # Calculate the number of components
            num_components, components = calculate_num_components(xvalues)

            # Add lazy constraint based on the number of components
            if num_components == 2:
                # Add a single constraint

                # labels of different components
                dfjeqns = np.zeros((1, FST_no), dtype=int)

                for i in range(FST_no):
                    currenth = T[i]

                    # currentcompv denotes all vertices in the current component
                    currentcompv = find(components, 0)
                    # notcurrentcompv denotes all vertices not in the current component
                    notcurrentcompv = list(set(range(terminal_no)) - set(currentcompv))

                    if len(set(currentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(currentcompv) and \
                            len(set(notcurrentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(notcurrentcompv):
                        dfjeqns[0, i] = 1

                model.cbLazy(gp.quicksum(model._vars[i] * dfjeqns[0, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1)

                # more than 2 components
            elif num_components >= 3:
                # labels of different components
                l = list(set(components))
                p = len(l)
                dfjeqns = np.zeros((p, FST_no), dtype=int)

                for i in range(FST_no):
                    currenth = T[i]

                    for j in range(p):
                        # currentcompv denotes all vertices in the current component
                        currentcompv = find(components, l[j])
                        # notcurrentcompv denotes all vertices not in the current component
                        notcurrentcompv = list(set(range(terminal_no)) - set(currentcompv))

                        if len(set(currentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(currentcompv) and \
                                len(set(notcurrentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(notcurrentcompv):
                            dfjeqns[j, i] = 1

                for comp in range(p):
                    # breakpoint()
                    # model.cbLazy(sum(x[i] * dfjeqns[comp, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1)
                    model.cbLazy(gp.quicksum(model._vars[i] * dfjeqns[comp, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1)
                # breakpoint()
                # model.cbLazy(sum(x[i] * dfjeqns[j, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1 for j in range(p))

    # Define a function to calculate the number of components based on x_values
    def calculate_num_components(x_values):
        # Implement your logic to calculate the number of components
        # based on the binary variable values (x_values)

        components = [i for i in range(len(N))]

        for i in range(len(x_values)):
            currenthyper = x_values[i]
            vincurrenthyper = T[currenthyper]

            comps = [components[i] for i in vincurrenthyper]
            aa = len(comps)
            compsnodouble = list(set(comps))
            ab = len(compsnodouble)

            nextitcomp = np.min(compsnodouble)

            for j in range(ab):
                if nextitcomp != compsnodouble[j]:
                    tempind = find(components, compsnodouble[j])
                    for k in tempind:
                        components[k] = nextitcomp

        # number of comps in the current graph
        num_components = len(list(set(components)))

        return num_components, components

    # Create a new model
    model = gp.Model()

    # Define variables
    x = model.addVars(FST_no, vtype=GRB.BINARY)

    # Set objective
    model.setObjective(sum(L[i] * x[i] for i in range(FST_no)), GRB.MINIMIZE)

    # Define regular constraints (not lazy)
    model.addConstr(sum(S[i] * x[i] for i in range(FST_no)) <= max_steiner_no)

    # Set up callback for lazy constraints
    model._vars = x  # Store variables in the model object for callback access
    model.params.LazyConstraints = 1  # Enable lazy constraints

    model.optimize(lazy_callback)

    xvalues = np.zeros((FST_no), dtype=int)
    for i in range(FST_no):
        xvalues[i] = x[i].x

    # Calculate the number of components
    num_components, components = calculate_num_components(xvalues)

    htree = [i for i in range(FST_no) if x[i].X > 0.5]

    F_star = []

    for index, htree_index in enumerate(htree):

        i = 0
        update_index = htree_index

        while update_index + 1 > len(FST_set[i]):
            update_index -= len(FST_set[i])
            i += 1

        fst = FST_set[i][update_index]
        F_star.append(fst)

    obj = model.getObjective()
    print(f"Objective Value is: {obj.getValue()}")
    return F_star


def concatenation(FST_set, terminal_no, max_steiner_no):
    concat_start = time.perf_counter()

    FST_no = 0

    for i in range(max_steiner_no + 1):
        FST_no += len(FST_set[i])

    print(f"total # of FSTs is {FST_no}")
    # number of Steiner points in each hyperedge
    S = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]
    # length of each hyperedge
    L = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]
    # terminals in each hyperedge
    T = [0 for i in range(FST_no)]

    FST_index = 0
    for i in range(max_steiner_no + 1):
        for fst in FST_set[i]:
            S[FST_index] = len(fst[0]) - len(fst[3])
            L[FST_index] = fst[4]
            T[FST_index] = fst[3]
            FST_index += 1

    # number of iterations
    iter = 1
    # number of cut-constraints implemented
    cutno = 0

    milp_model = gp.Model("milp")
    milp_model.Params.LogToConsole = 0

    x = milp_model.addVars(FST_no, vtype=GRB.BINARY)

    milp_model.setObjective(sum(L[i] * x[i] for i in range(FST_no)), GRB.MINIMIZE)
    c2 = milp_model.addConstr(sum(S[i] * x[i] for i in range(FST_no)) <= max_steiner_no)

    if compatibility_test_on == True:

        for i in range(terminal_no - 1):
            for j in range(i + 1, terminal_no):

                common_terminals_list = []

                for ind_fst in range(FST_no):
                    if i in T[ind_fst] and j in T[ind_fst]:
                        common_terminals_list.append(ind_fst)

                milp_model.addConstr(sum(x[i] for i in common_terminals_list) <= 1.5)
                print(f"{len(common_terminals_list)} FSTs share terminals {i} and {j}")

    milp_model.optimize()

    xvalues = np.zeros((FST_no), dtype=int)
    for i in range(FST_no):
        xvalues[i] = x[i].x

    print(f"Iteration #{iter} and {cutno} out of a possible {2 ** (terminal_no - 1) - 1} cut-constraints implemented")
    components = [i for i in range(terminal_no)]

    hypertree = find(xvalues, 1)
    for i in range(len(hypertree)):
        currenthyper = hypertree[i]
        vincurrenthyper = T[currenthyper]

        comps = [components[i] for i in vincurrenthyper]
        aa = len(comps)
        compsnodouble = list(set(comps))
        ab = len(compsnodouble)

        nextitcomp = np.min(compsnodouble)

        for j in range(ab):
            if nextitcomp != compsnodouble[j]:
                tempind = find(components, compsnodouble[j])
                for k in tempind:
                    components[k] = nextitcomp

    # number of comps in the current graph
    noofcomps = len(list(set(components)))

    while noofcomps > 1.5:

        if noofcomps == 2:
            # labels of different components
            dfjeqns = np.zeros((1, FST_no), dtype=int)
            cutno = cutno + 1

            for i in range(FST_no):
                currenth = T[i]

                # currentcompv denotes all vertices in the current component
                currentcompv = find(components, 0)
                # notcurrentcompv denotes all vertices not in the current component
                notcurrentcompv = list(set(range(terminal_no)) - set(currentcompv))

                if len(set(currentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(currentcompv) and \
                        len(set(notcurrentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(notcurrentcompv):
                    dfjeqns[0, i] = 1

            milp_model.addConstr(sum(x[i] * dfjeqns[0, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1)

        elif noofcomps >= 3:
            # labels of different components
            l = list(set(components))
            p = len(l)
            dfjeqns = np.zeros((p, FST_no), dtype=int)
            cutno = cutno + noofcomps

            for i in range(FST_no):
                currenth = T[i]

                for j in range(p):
                    # currentcompv denotes all vertices in the current component
                    currentcompv = find(components, l[j])
                    # notcurrentcompv denotes all vertices not in the current component
                    notcurrentcompv = list(set(range(terminal_no)) - set(currentcompv))

                    if len(set(currentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(currentcompv) and \
                            len(set(notcurrentcompv) - set(currenth)) < len(notcurrentcompv):
                        dfjeqns[j, i] = 1

            milp_model.addConstrs(sum(x[i] * dfjeqns[j, i] for i in range(FST_no)) >= 1 for j in range(p))

        iter = iter + 1
        milp_model.optimize()

        xvalues = np.zeros((FST_no), dtype=int)
        for i in range(FST_no):
            xvalues[i] = x[i].x

        print(
            f"Iteration #{iter} and {cutno} out of a possible {2 ** (terminal_no - 1) - 1} cut-constraints implemented")
        components = [i for i in range(terminal_no)]

        hypertree = find(xvalues, 1)
        for i in range(len(hypertree)):
            currenthyper = hypertree[i]
            vincurrenthyper = T[currenthyper]

            comps = [components[i] for i in vincurrenthyper]
            aa = len(comps)
            compsnodouble = list(set(comps))
            ab = len(compsnodouble)

            nextitcomp = np.min(compsnodouble)

            for j in range(ab):
                if nextitcomp != compsnodouble[j]:
                    tempind = find(components, compsnodouble[j])
                    for k in tempind:
                        components[k] = nextitcomp

        # number of comps in the current graph
        noofcomps = len(list(set(components)))

    htree = find(xvalues, 1)
    F_star = []
    for htree_index in htree:

        i = 0
        update_index = htree_index

        while update_index + 1 > len(FST_set[i]):
            update_index -= len(FST_set[i])
            i += 1

        fst = FST_set[i][update_index]
        F_star.append(fst)

    obj = milp_model.getObjective()
    print(f"Objective Value is: {obj.getValue()}")

    return F_star


def reverse_melzak_for_F(F):
    for F_i in F:
        for fst in F_i:

            fst_vertices = fst[0]
            number_of_steiner_pts = 0

            steiner_index = [-1 for i in range(len(fst_vertices))]

            for index, vertex in enumerate(fst_vertices):
                if vertex[2] < - 0.5:
                    steiner_index[index] = number_of_steiner_pts
                    number_of_steiner_pts += 1

            # print(f"before: fst with {number_of_steiner_pts} steiner pts: {fst_vertices}")

            if number_of_steiner_pts == 0:
                continue

            if number_of_steiner_pts == 1:
                reverse_melzak_one(fst)
                # print(f"after: fst_vertices {fst[0]}")
            elif number_of_steiner_pts == 2:
                reverse_melzak_two(fst)
                # print(f"after: fst_vertices {fst[0]}")
            else:
                reverse_melzak_general(fst, number_of_steiner_pts)


def reverse_melzak_one(fst_one):
    fst_vertices = fst_one[0]

    # a single deg-4 steiner pt
    if len(fst_vertices) == 5:
        t1 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
        t2 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
        t3 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
        t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
        steiner = line_intersection([t1, t3], [t2, t4], 0, 0)

        fst_vertices[3][0] = steiner[0]
        fst_vertices[3][1] = steiner[1]

    # a single deg-3 steiner pt
    else:
        t1 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
        t2 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
        t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]

        eq = eqpoint(t1, t2)
        new_arc_centre = [(t1[0] + t2[0] + eq[0]) / 3,
                          (t1[1] + t2[1] + eq[1]) / 3]
        new_arc_radius = dist(t1, new_arc_centre)
        new_arc_angle1 = angle(t1, eq)
        new_arc_angle2 = angle(t2, eq)
        arc_t1_t2 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

        steiner = line_arc_intersection(eq, t3, 0, [eq, arc_t1_t2])

        fst_vertices[2][0] = steiner[0]
        fst_vertices[2][1] = steiner[1]

    return


def reverse_melzak_two(fst_two):
    fst_vertices = fst_two[0]
    parents = [vertex[3] for vertex in fst_vertices]

    # two deg-3 steiner pts
    if len(fst_vertices) == 6:

        for ind, vertex in enumerate(fst_vertices):
            if vertex[2] == -1:
                s1_index = ind
                break

        if parents[0] == 4:
            t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
            t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
            t3 = [fst_vertices[5][0], fst_vertices[5][1]]
            t4 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
        else:
            t1 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
            t2 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
            t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
            t4 = [fst_vertices[5][0], fst_vertices[5][1]]

        eq12 = eqpoint(t1, t2)
        eq34 = eqpoint(t3, t4)

        # find the second steiner pt
        new_arc_centre = [(t3[0] + t4[0] + eq34[0]) / 3,
                          (t3[1] + t4[1] + eq34[1]) / 3]
        new_arc_radius = dist(t3, new_arc_centre)
        new_arc_angle1 = angle(t3, eq34)
        new_arc_angle2 = angle(t4, eq34)
        arc_t3_t4 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

        steiner34 = line_arc_intersection(eq12, eq34, 0, [eq34, arc_t3_t4])

        fst_vertices[4][0] = steiner34[0]
        fst_vertices[4][1] = steiner34[1]

        # find the first steiner pt
        new_arc_centre = [(t1[0] + t2[0] + eq12[0]) / 3,
                          (t1[1] + t2[1] + eq12[1]) / 3]
        new_arc_radius = dist(t1, new_arc_centre)
        new_arc_angle1 = angle(t1, eq12)
        new_arc_angle2 = angle(t2, eq12)
        arc_t1_t2 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

        steiner12 = line_arc_intersection(eq12, eq34, 0, [eq12, arc_t1_t2])

        fst_vertices[s1_index][0] = steiner12[0]
        fst_vertices[s1_index][1] = steiner12[1]

    # one deg-3 steiner pt and one deg-4 steiner pt
    elif len(fst_vertices) == 7:

        # second steiner pt is of deg 3
        if fst_vertices[5][2] == -1:
            if fst_vertices[4][2] == -2:

                t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
                t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
                t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
                t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
                t6 = [fst_vertices[6][0], fst_vertices[6][1]]

                eq60 = eqpoint(t6, t0)

                # find the second steiner pt
                new_arc_centre = [(t0[0] + t6[0] + eq60[0]) / 3,
                                  (t0[1] + t6[1] + eq60[1]) / 3]
                new_arc_radius = dist(t0, new_arc_centre)
                new_arc_angle1 = angle(t6, eq60)
                new_arc_angle2 = angle(t0, eq60)
                arc_t6_t0 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

                steiner60 = line_arc_intersection(t2, eq60, 0, [eq60, arc_t6_t0])

                fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner60[0]
                fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner60[1]

                # find the first steiner pt

                steiner4 = line_intersection([eq60, t2], [t1, t3], 0, 0)

                fst_vertices[4][0] = steiner4[0]
                fst_vertices[4][1] = steiner4[1]

            else:
                t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
                t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
                t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
                t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
                t6 = [fst_vertices[6][0], fst_vertices[6][1]]

                eq46 = eqpoint(t4, t6)

                # find the second steiner pt
                new_arc_centre = [(t4[0] + t6[0] + eq46[0]) / 3,
                                  (t4[1] + t6[1] + eq46[1]) / 3]
                new_arc_radius = dist(t4, new_arc_centre)
                new_arc_angle1 = angle(t4, eq46)
                new_arc_angle2 = angle(t6, eq46)
                arc_t4_t6 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

                steiner46 = line_arc_intersection(t1, eq46, 0, [eq46, arc_t4_t6])

                fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner46[0]
                fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner46[1]

                # find the first steiner pt

                steiner3 = line_intersection([eq46, t1], [t0, t2], 0, 0)

                fst_vertices[3][0] = steiner3[0]
                fst_vertices[3][1] = steiner3[1]

        # second steiner pt is of deg 4
        else:
            if fst_vertices[4][2] == -1:
                t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
                t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
                t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
                t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
                t6 = [fst_vertices[6][0], fst_vertices[6][1]]

                eq23 = eqpoint(t2, t3)

                # find the second steiner pt

                steiner5 = line_intersection([eq23, t0], [t1, t6], 0, 0)

                fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner5[0]
                fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner5[1]

                # find the first steiner pt

                new_arc_centre = [(t2[0] + t3[0] + eq23[0]) / 3,
                                  (t2[1] + t3[1] + eq23[1]) / 3]
                new_arc_radius = dist(t2, new_arc_centre)
                new_arc_angle1 = angle(t2, eq23)
                new_arc_angle2 = angle(t3, eq23)
                arc_t2_t3 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

                steiner23 = line_arc_intersection(t0, eq23, 0, [eq23, arc_t2_t3])

                fst_vertices[4][0] = steiner23[0]
                fst_vertices[4][1] = steiner23[1]

            elif fst_vertices[3][2] == -1:
                t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
                t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
                t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
                t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
                t6 = [fst_vertices[6][0], fst_vertices[6][1]]

                eq12 = eqpoint(t1, t2)

                # find the second steiner pt

                steiner5 = line_intersection([eq12, t6], [t0, t4], 0, 0)

                fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner5[0]
                fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner5[1]

                # find the first steiner pt

                new_arc_centre = [(t1[0] + t2[0] + eq12[0]) / 3,
                                  (t1[1] + t2[1] + eq12[1]) / 3]
                new_arc_radius = dist(t1, new_arc_centre)
                new_arc_angle1 = angle(t1, eq12)
                new_arc_angle2 = angle(t2, eq12)
                arc_t1_t2 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

                steiner12 = line_arc_intersection(t6, eq12, 0, [eq12, arc_t1_t2])

                fst_vertices[3][0] = steiner12[0]
                fst_vertices[3][1] = steiner12[1]

            else:
                t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
                t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
                t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
                t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
                t6 = [fst_vertices[6][0], fst_vertices[6][1]]

                eq01 = eqpoint(t0, t1)

                # find the second steiner pt

                steiner5 = line_intersection([eq01, t4], [t3, t6], 0, 0)

                fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner5[0]
                fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner5[1]

                # find the first steiner pt

                new_arc_centre = [(t0[0] + t1[0] + eq01[0]) / 3,
                                  (t0[1] + t1[1] + eq01[1]) / 3]
                new_arc_radius = dist(t0, new_arc_centre)
                new_arc_angle1 = angle(t0, eq01)
                new_arc_angle2 = angle(t1, eq01)
                arc_t0_t1 = [new_arc_centre[0], new_arc_centre[1], new_arc_radius, new_arc_angle1, new_arc_angle2]

                steiner01 = line_arc_intersection(t4, eq01, 0, [eq01, arc_t0_t1])

                fst_vertices[2][0] = steiner01[0]
                fst_vertices[2][1] = steiner01[1]

    # two deg-4 steiner pts
    else:

        if fst_vertices[4][2] == -2:

            eq1 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
            eq2 = [fst_vertices[7][0], fst_vertices[7][1]]

            t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]
            t3 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
            t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
            t5 = [fst_vertices[5][0], fst_vertices[5][1]]

            steiner4 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t1, t3], 0, 0)
            steiner6 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t0, t5], 0, 0)

            fst_vertices[4][0] = steiner4[0]
            fst_vertices[4][1] = steiner4[1]

            fst_vertices[6][0] = steiner6[0]
            fst_vertices[6][1] = steiner6[1]

        elif fst_vertices[3][2] == -2:

            eq1 = [fst_vertices[5][0], fst_vertices[5][1]]
            eq2 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]

            t7 = [fst_vertices[7][0], fst_vertices[7][1]]
            t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
            t0 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]
            t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]

            steiner6 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t7, t4], 0, 0)
            steiner3 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t0, t2], 0, 0)

            fst_vertices[6][0] = steiner6[0]
            fst_vertices[6][1] = steiner6[1]

            fst_vertices[3][0] = steiner3[0]
            fst_vertices[3][1] = steiner3[1]

        else:

            eq1 = [fst_vertices[3][0], fst_vertices[3][1]]
            eq2 = [fst_vertices[0][0], fst_vertices[0][1]]

            t4 = [fst_vertices[4][0], fst_vertices[4][1]]
            t2 = [fst_vertices[2][0], fst_vertices[2][1]]
            t7 = [fst_vertices[7][0], fst_vertices[7][1]]
            t1 = [fst_vertices[1][0], fst_vertices[1][1]]

            steiner5 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t4, t2], 0, 0)
            steiner6 = line_intersection([eq1, eq2], [t7, t1], 0, 0)

            fst_vertices[5][0] = steiner5[0]
            fst_vertices[5][1] = steiner5[1]

            fst_vertices[6][0] = steiner6[0]
            fst_vertices[6][1] = steiner6[1]

    return


def reverse_melzak_general(fst_general, steiner_number):
    fst_vertices = fst_general[0]

    vertex_no = len(fst_vertices)

    steiner_finder = vertex_no - 2

    for i in range(steiner_number):

        while fst_vertices[steiner_finder][2] > -0.5:
            steiner_finder -= 1

        steiner_of_interest = fst_vertices[steiner_finder]

        parent_of_steiner = fst_vertices[steiner_of_interest[3]]

        # steiner pt of interest lies on an arc
        if len(steiner_of_interest[1]) == 7:

            steiner_location = line_arc_intersection(steiner_of_interest[0],
                                                     [parent_of_steiner[0], parent_of_steiner[1]], 0,
                                                     [steiner_of_interest[0], steiner_of_interest[1]])
            steiner_of_interest[0] = steiner_location[0]
            steiner_of_interest[1] = steiner_location[1]

        # steiner pt of interest lies on a segment
        else:

            steiner_location = line_intersection([steiner_of_interest[0], [parent_of_steiner[0], parent_of_steiner[1]]],
                                                 [[steiner_of_interest[1][0], steiner_of_interest[1][1]],
                                                  [steiner_of_interest[1][2], steiner_of_interest[1][3]]], 0, 0)

            steiner_of_interest[0] = steiner_location[0]
            steiner_of_interest[1] = steiner_location[1]

        steiner_finder -= 1


def reverse_melzak(final_solution):
    for fst in final_solution:
        fst_vertices = fst[0]
        number_of_steiner_pts = 0

        steiner_index = [-1 for i in range(len(fst_vertices))]

        for index, vertex in enumerate(fst_vertices):
            if vertex[2] < - 0.5:
                steiner_index[index] = number_of_steiner_pts
                number_of_steiner_pts += 1

        if number_of_steiner_pts == 0:
            continue

        if number_of_steiner_pts == 1:
            reverse_melzak_one(fst)
        elif number_of_steiner_pts == 2:
            reverse_melzak_two(fst)
        else:
            reverse_melzak_general(fst, number_of_steiner_pts)

    return


def interval_plot(b1):
    # given a branch b1, this function plots its pseudoterminal and its arc or segment

    colour = list(np.random.uniform(0, 1, 3))

    # pseudoterminal
    plt.plot(b1[1][0], b1[1][1], marker='o', markersize=6,
             markeredgecolor=colour, markerfacecolor=colour)
    # arc
    if len(b1[2]) == 7:
        if b1[2][6] >= b1[2][5]:
            angles = np.linspace(b1[2][5], b1[2][6])
        else:
            angles = np.linspace(b1[2][5], b1[2][6] + 2 * np.pi)

        x = [b1[2][0] + b1[2][2] * math.cos(theta) for theta in angles]
        y = [b1[2][1] + b1[2][2] * math.sin(theta) for theta in angles]
        plt.scatter(x, y, color=colour, marker='o', s=5)

        # rays
        x_r = np.linspace(b1[1][0], 3 * (b1[2][0] + b1[2][2] * math.cos(b1[2][5]) - b1[1][0]) + b1[1][0])
        y_r = np.linspace(b1[1][1], 3 * (b1[2][1] + b1[2][2] * math.sin(b1[2][5]) - b1[1][1]) + b1[1][1])
        x_l = np.linspace(b1[1][0], 3 * (b1[2][0] + b1[2][2] * math.cos(b1[2][6]) - b1[1][0]) + b1[1][0])
        y_l = np.linspace(b1[1][1], 3 * (b1[2][1] + b1[2][2] * math.sin(b1[2][6]) - b1[1][1]) + b1[1][1])
        plt.scatter(x_r, y_r, color=colour, marker='o', s=5)
        plt.scatter(x_l, y_l, color=colour, marker='o', s=5)

    # segment
    else:
        x = np.linspace(b1[2][0], b1[2][2])
        y = np.linspace(b1[2][1], b1[2][3])
        plt.scatter(x, y, color=colour, marker='o', s=15)

        x_r = np.linspace(b1[1][0], b1[1][0] + 3 * (b1[2][0] - b1[1][0]))
        y_r = np.linspace(b1[1][1], b1[1][1] + 3 * (b1[2][1] - b1[1][1]))
        x_l = np.linspace(b1[1][0], b1[1][0] + 3 * (b1[2][2] - b1[1][0]))
        y_l = np.linspace(b1[1][1], b1[1][1] + 3 * (b1[2][3] - b1[1][1]))
        plt.scatter(x_r, y_r, color=colour, marker='o', s=5)
        plt.scatter(x_l, y_l, color=colour, marker='o', s=5)

    plt.axis('scaled')
    return


def cluster_plot(cluster, N):
    vertexlist = cluster[0]
    vertexno = len(cluster[0])

    for j in range(vertexno - 1):
        plt.plot([vertexlist[j][0], vertexlist[vertexlist[j][3]][0]],
                 [vertexlist[j][1], vertexlist[vertexlist[j][3]][1]], color='b')

    for terminal_index in range(len(N)):
        plt.plot(N[terminal_index][0], N[terminal_index][1], marker='s', markersize=5,
                 markeredgecolor="k", markerfacecolor='k')

    for i in range(vertexno):
        if vertexlist[i][2] == -1:
            plt.plot(vertexlist[i][0], vertexlist[i][1],
                     marker='o', markersize=5, markeredgecolor="r", markerfacecolor='r')
        elif vertexlist[i][2] == -2:
            plt.plot(vertexlist[i][0], vertexlist[i][1],
                     marker='o', markersize=5, markeredgecolor="m", markerfacecolor='m')

    plt.axis('scaled')
    plt.axis([0, 1, 0, 1])
    # plt.show()
    return


def k_steiner(N, k):
    alg_start = time.perf_counter()
    n = len(N)
    print(f"n = {n} and k = {k}")

    # B is the set of branches where B[0] is the set of branches with 0 steiner pt, ...
    B = [[] for i in range(k + 1)]

    # F is the set of FSTs where F[0] is the set of FSTs with 0 steiner pt, ...
    F = [[] for i in range(k + 1)]

    # Generate branches of size 0 (terminals)
    for terminal_no, terminal in enumerate(N[:-1]):
        B[0].append([[[terminal[0], terminal[1], terminal_no, -1]], terminal,
                     [terminal[0], terminal[1], 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], [terminal_no], 0])

    # Generate the MST and FSTs of size 0 (edges of the MST)
    mst = MST(N)
    for [u, v] in mst:
        edge_length = dist(N[u], N[v])
        F[0].append([[[N[u][0], N[u][1], u, 1], [N[v][0], N[v][1], v, -1]], -1, -1, [u, v], edge_length])
        # plt.plot([N[u][0], N[v][0]], [N[u][1], N[v][1]])

    # plt.show()

    if double_bottle_on == True or triple_bottle_on == True:
        global bottleneck_bound_array
        bottleneck_bound_array = bottleneck_creator(mst)

        global bst
        global EPS
        global EPS2
        EPS = 10 ** (-6)
        EPS2 = 10 ** (-7)
        edges = kruskalMST(N)
        bst = bottleneckSteinerDistance(N, edges)

    if wedge_on == True:
        global hull_vertices
        hull_vertices = ConvexHull(np.array(N)).vertices

    # Generate branches and FSTs of size current_k
    for current_k in range(1, k + 1):
        print(f"Starting {current_k} Steiner pt generation - double merge")
        # double merging
        for k_1 in range(math.floor((current_k - 1) / 2) + 1):
            for index_1, branch_1 in enumerate(B[k_1]):

                # branch_1 and branch_2 have the same number of steiner pts
                if k_1 == current_k - k_1 - 1:
                    for index_2 in range(index_1):
                        branch_2 = B[current_k - k_1 - 1][index_2]
                        if any(x in branch_1[3] for x in branch_2[3]) is False:
                            # and max(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3]) < n - 1:
                            branch_12 = double_merge(branch_1, branch_2)
                            if branch_12 != 0:
                                B[current_k].append(branch_12)
                            branch_21 = double_merge(branch_2, branch_1)
                            if branch_21 != 0:
                                B[current_k].append(branch_21)

                # branch_1 and branch_2 have a different number of steiner pts
                else:
                    for index_2, branch_2 in enumerate(B[current_k - k_1 - 1]):
                        if any(x in branch_1[3] for x in branch_2[3]) is False:
                            # and max(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3]) < n - 1:
                            branch_12 = double_merge(branch_1, branch_2)
                            if branch_12 != 0:
                                B[current_k].append(branch_12)
                            branch_21 = double_merge(branch_2, branch_1)
                            if branch_21 != 0:
                                B[current_k].append(branch_21)

        # triple merging
        print(f"Starting {current_k} Steiner pt generation - triple merge")
        for k_3 in range(math.ceil((current_k - 1) / 3), current_k):
            for index_3, branch_3 in enumerate(B[k_3]):
                for k_2 in range(math.ceil((current_k - 1 - k_3) / 2), k_3 + 1):
                    k_1 = current_k - 1 - k_3 - k_2
                    if k_1 < 0:
                        continue

                    if k_3 > k_2 > k_1:
                        for index_2, branch_2 in enumerate(B[k_2]):
                            for index_1, branch_1 in enumerate(B[k_1]):
                                if (len(list(set(branch_1[3]) | set(branch_2[3]) | set(branch_3[3]))) ==
                                        len(branch_1[3]) + len(branch_2[3]) + len(branch_3[3])):
                                    # and max(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3] + branch_3[3]) < n-1:
                                    order_triple = triple_merge_checker(branch_1, branch_2, branch_3, current_k)
                                    if order_triple != 0:
                                        branches = [branch_1, branch_2, branch_3]
                                        for order in order_triple:
                                            temp_branch = triple_merge(branches[order[0]], branches[order[1]],
                                                                       branches[order[2]])
                                            if temp_branch != 0:
                                                if len(temp_branch) == 2:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[0])
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[1])
                                                else:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch)
                    elif k_3 == k_2 > k_1:
                        for index_2 in range(index_3):
                            branch_2 = B[k_2][index_2]
                            for index_1, branch_1 in enumerate(B[k_1]):
                                if (len(list(set(branch_1[3]) | set(branch_2[3]) | set(branch_3[3]))) ==
                                        len(branch_1[3]) + len(branch_2[3]) + len(branch_3[3])):
                                    # and len(set(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3] + branch_3[3])) < n:
                                    order_triple = triple_merge_checker(branch_1, branch_2, branch_3, current_k)
                                    if order_triple != 0:
                                        branches = [branch_1, branch_2, branch_3]
                                        for order in order_triple:
                                            temp_branch = triple_merge(branches[order[0]], branches[order[1]],
                                                                       branches[order[2]])
                                            if temp_branch != 0:
                                                if len(temp_branch) == 2:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[0])
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[1])
                                                else:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch)
                    elif k_3 > k_2 == k_1:
                        for index_2, branch_2 in enumerate(B[k_2]):
                            for index_1 in range(index_2):
                                branch_1 = B[k_1][index_1]
                                if (len(list(set(branch_1[3]) | set(branch_2[3]) | set(branch_3[3]))) == len(
                                        branch_1[3]) + len(branch_2[3]) + len(branch_3[3])):
                                    # and len(set(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3] + branch_3[3])) < n):
                                    order_triple = triple_merge_checker(branch_1, branch_2, branch_3, current_k)
                                    if order_triple != 0:
                                        branches = [branch_1, branch_2, branch_3]
                                        for order in order_triple:
                                            temp_branch = triple_merge(branches[order[0]], branches[order[1]],
                                                                       branches[order[2]])
                                            if temp_branch != 0:
                                                if len(temp_branch) == 2:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[0])
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[1])
                                                else:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch)

                    # k_3 == k_2 == k_1
                    else:
                        for index_2 in range(index_3):
                            for index_1 in range(index_2):
                                branch_2 = B[k_2][index_2]
                                branch_1 = B[k_1][index_1]
                                if (len(list(set(branch_1[3]) | set(branch_2[3]) | set(branch_3[3]))) == len(
                                        branch_1[3]) + len(branch_2[3]) + len(branch_3[3])):
                                    # and len(set(branch_1[3] + branch_2[3] + branch_3[3])) < n):
                                    order_triple = triple_merge_checker(branch_1, branch_2, branch_3, current_k)
                                    if order_triple != 0:
                                        branches = [branch_1, branch_2, branch_3]
                                        for order in order_triple:
                                            temp_branch = triple_merge(branches[order[0]], branches[order[1]],
                                                                       branches[order[2]])
                                            if temp_branch != 0:
                                                if len(temp_branch) == 2:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[0])
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch[1])
                                                else:
                                                    B[current_k].append(temp_branch)

        # termination
        print(f"Starting {current_k} Steiner pt generation - termination")
        for branch_1 in B[current_k]:

            temp_list = [i for i in range(2, n)]
            max_index = max(branch_1[3])
            unvisited = [x for x in temp_list if x > max_index]

            for terminal in unvisited:
                result = termination_merge(branch_1, N, terminal)

                # result != 0 when termination can occur
                if result != 0:
                    F[current_k].append(result)

    branch_no = [0 for ii in range(k + 1)]
    fst_no = [0 for ii in range(k + 1)]

    gen_end = time.perf_counter()
    gen_time = gen_end - alg_start
    print(f"time taken for generation phase is {gen_time}")

    pre_fst_no = 0

    pre_deg_three_no = 0
    pre_deg_four_no = 0
    pre_fst_containing_deg4 = 0

    for F_i in F:
        pre_fst_no += len(F_i)

        if print_deg4_additional_stats == True:

            for fst in F_i:

                pre_fst_contains_deg4 = 0

                for vertex in fst[0]:
                    if vertex[2] == -1:
                        pre_deg_three_no += 1
                    elif vertex[2] == -2:
                        pre_deg_four_no += 1
                        pre_fst_contains_deg4 = 1

                if pre_fst_contains_deg4 == 1:
                    pre_fst_containing_deg4 += 1

    post_start = time.perf_counter()

    if post_proc_on == True:

        reverse_melzak_for_F(F)

        if post_lune_on == True:
            post_lune_test(F)

        if post_bottle_on == True:
            post_bottle_test(F)

    post_end = time.perf_counter()
    post_time = post_end - post_start
    print(f"time taken for post-processing is {post_time}")

    deg_three_no = 0
    deg_four_no = 0
    fst_containing_deg4 = 0

    for i in range(k + 1):
        branch_no[i] = len(B[i])
        fst_no[i] = len(F[i])
        print("number of branches with", i, "steiner pts is", len(B[i]))

        if print_deg4_additional_stats == True:

            for fst in F[i]:

                fst_contains_deg4 = 0

                for vertex in fst[0]:
                    if vertex[2] == -1:
                        deg_three_no += 1
                    elif vertex[2] == -2:
                        deg_four_no += 1
                        fst_contains_deg4 = 1

                if fst_contains_deg4 == 1:
                    fst_containing_deg4 += 1
        """
        if i == 1:
            for fst in F[i]:
                print(f"fst with k = 1, n = {len(fst[0])}, {fst}")
                reverse_melzak_one(fst)
                cluster_plot(fst, N)
                print(f"length of fst is: {fst[4]}")
                plt.show()
            for branch in B[i]:
                print("branch", branch)

        if i == 2:
            for fst in F[i]:
                print(f"2-fst with {len(fst[0])} vertices, {fst}")
                reverse_melzak_two(fst)
                cluster_plot(fst, N)
                print(f"length of fst is: {fst[4]}")
                plt.show()

        if i > 2:
            for fst in F[i]:
                reverse_melzak_general(fst, i)
                cluster_plot(fst, N)
                plt.show()
        """

        print("number of FSTs with", i, "steiner pts is", len(F[i]))

        # for fst in F[i]:
        # print("fst", fst)

    concat_start = time.perf_counter()
    opt = concatenation_lazy(F, n, k)

    # print(f"FINAL opt soln is : {opt}")
    concat_end = time.perf_counter()
    concat_time = concat_end - concat_start
    print(f"time taken for concatenation is {concat_time}")

    obj_value = 0
    for optimal_fst in opt:
        obj_value += optimal_fst[4]

    if revmelzak_on == True:

        if post_proc_on != True:
            reverse_melzak(opt)
            end = time.perf_counter()
            time_after_revmelzak = end - start
            print(f"time taken for n={len(N)}, k={k}: {time_after_revmelzak}")

        if plot_on == True:
            for fst in opt:
                cluster_plot(fst, N)

            plt.show()

    print(f"opt: {opt}")
    return opt, obj_value, branch_no, pre_fst_no, fst_no, gen_time, post_time, concat_time, [pre_deg_three_no, deg_three_no], [pre_deg_four_no, deg_four_no], [pre_fst_containing_deg4, fst_containing_deg4]  # , total_time_taken


print(f"----------------------------")

        time_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        gen_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        post_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        concat_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]

        pre_deg_three_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        deg_three_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        pre_deg_four_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        deg_four_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        pre_deg_four_fsts_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        deg_four_fsts_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        obj_value_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        branch_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        pre_fst_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]
        fst_list = [0 for i in range(len(N_total))]

        for iter, N in enumerate(N_total):
            print(f"iteration number {iter + 1}")
            start = time.perf_counter()
            soln, obj_value, branch_no, pre_fst_no, fst_no, gen_time, post_time, concat_time, [pre_deg_three_no, deg_three_no], [pre_deg_four_no, deg_four_no], [pre_deg4_fsts, deg4_fsts] = k_steiner(N, k)
            end = time.perf_counter()
            # print(f"solution is {soln}")
            gen_list[iter] = gen_time
            post_list[iter] = post_time
            concat_list[iter] = concat_time
            time_list[iter] = end - start
            pre_deg_three_list[iter] = pre_deg_three_no
            deg_three_list[iter] = deg_three_no
            pre_deg_four_list[iter] = pre_deg_four_no
            deg_four_list[iter] = deg_four_no
            pre_deg_four_fsts_list[iter] = pre_deg4_fsts
            deg_four_fsts_list[iter] = deg4_fsts
            obj_value_list[iter] = obj_value
            branch_list[iter] = sum(branch_no)
            pre_fst_list[iter] = pre_fst_no
            fst_list[iter] = sum(fst_no)

        print(f"----------------------------")
        print(f"time taken: {[round(i, 5) for i in time_list]}")
        print(f"average time taken: {round(np.mean(time_list), 5)}")
        print(f"gen time taken: {[round(i, 5) for i in gen_list]}")
        print(f"average gen time taken: {round(np.mean(gen_list), 5)}")
        print(f"post time taken: {[round(i, 5) for i in post_list]}")
        print(f"average post time taken: {round(np.mean(post_list), 5)}")
        print(f"concat time taken: {[round(i, 5) for i in concat_list]}")
        print(f"average concat time taken: {round(np.mean(concat_list), 5)}")

            print(f"number of pre-deg 3 steiner pts: {pre_deg_three_list}")
            print(f"average no of pre-deg 3 steiner pts: {np.mean(pre_deg_three_list)}")

            print(f"number of deg 3 steiner pts: {deg_three_list}")
            print(f"average no of deg 3 steiner pts: {np.mean(deg_three_list)}")

            print(f"number of pre-deg 4 steiner pts: {pre_deg_four_list}")
            print(f"average no of pre-deg 4 steiner pts: {np.mean(pre_deg_four_list)}")

            print(f"number of deg 4 steiner pts: {deg_four_list}")
            print(f"average no of deg 4 steiner pts: {np.mean(deg_four_list)}")

            print(f"number of pre-fsts with deg 4 steiner points: {pre_deg_four_fsts_list}")
            print(f"average no of pre-fsts with deg 4 steiner points: {np.mean(pre_deg_four_fsts_list)}")

            print(f"number of fsts with deg 4 steiner points: {deg_four_fsts_list}")
            print(f"average no of fsts with deg 4 steiner points: {np.mean(deg_four_fsts_list)}")

        # print(f"objective function values:{obj_value_list}")
        print(f"number of branches: {branch_list}")
        print(f"average number of branches: {np.mean(branch_list)}")
        print(f"number of pre-fsts: {pre_fst_list}")
        print(f"average number of pre-fsts: {np.mean(pre_fst_list)}")
        print(f"number of fsts: {fst_list}")
        print(f"average number of fsts: {np.mean(fst_list)}")

        continue_test = input("Continue testing?")
